2010 Audit Report, Financial Statements And Notes Of Bank Of Chongqing Co., Ltd. ..... 125

2010年是中国经济极其不平凡的一年，面对国际、国内复杂多变的经济金融形势，中国政府出台了一系列强有力的政策措施，有效巩固和扩大了应对国际金融危机冲击的成果，国民经济运行态势总体良好。重庆市在市委、市政府的正确领导下，积极贯彻落实“314”总体部署和中央宏观调控要求，主动适应国内外形势变化，以内陆开放高地和金融中心建设、民生工程、“两翼”农户万元增收工程等项目为抓手，扩内需、调结构、促增长、惠民生，不断改善经济发展环境，推动经济社会实现了持续、快速、健康发展。

本行紧紧抓住国家经济企稳向好和地方经济强劲发展的机遇，围绕“五个重庆”建设，在市委、市政府的领导和监管机构的引领下，坚持积极审慎的经营方针，沉着应对各项挑战，在全行员工的共同努力下，资产规模突破千亿元大关，经营业绩再创历史新高。截止2010年末，资产总额1082亿元，存款余额739亿元，贷款余额530亿元，实现净利润10.8亿元，资本充足率12.41%，不良贷款拨备覆盖率534.45%，不良贷款率0.36%，完成了年初董事会确定的经营目标，实现了规模、质量、效益的同步增长。

展望2011年，全球经济将继续温和复苏，经济发展的外部环境略好于2010年；同时，今年是“十二五”规划的开局之年，国家实施积极的财政政策和稳健的货币政策，促使经济运行回归到正常增长轨道，推动经济结构调整和发展方式转变将成为新的课题，也给本行未来发展带来许多新的机遇和挑战。在新的一年里，我们将继续以“科学发展为主题、以加快转变发展方式为主线”，坚持“防风险、调结构、保增长、促转变”的指导思想，坚持为地方经济、中小企业和城市居民的服务方向，以培育上市为目标，不断完善公司治理、优化组织架构、强化风险管理、提升内部控制，大力改进金融服务，增强竞争发展能力、风险管控能力和可持续盈利能力，努力为股东、为客户、为社会创造更大的价值。

最后，我谨代表重庆银行董事会向关心和支持本行发展的各级领导、各位股东、社会各界朋友和忠实的客户表示最诚挚的谢意！向辛勤劳动、尽职敬业的全体员工表示亲切的慰问！重庆银行将在2011年里再接再厉、续写新的辉煌！

董事长：马海

二〇一一年二月十一日

The year of 2010 witnessed an extraordinary China economy. For the capricious economy and finance at home and abroad, Chinese government promulgated effective policies and measures to calm the international financial turmoil and well control the national economy. Guided by CPC Chongqing Municipal Committee and Chongqing Municipal People's Government, Chongqing made every effort to realize the prospect portrayed by the Central Government. It positively acclimatized itself to the changes at home and abroad and relied on opening-up and finance, people's livelihood promotion, farmers' income increase and so on. Chongqing also unceasingly improved its economic environment through various measures to sustainably and soundly develop the economic society.

For favorable opportunities, positive prudent operation, staff's joint efforts and the guidance of the government, the asset size of the Bank exceeded a hundred billion yuan. By the end of 2010, the general assets had been 108.2 billion yuan; outstanding of deposits, 73.9 billion yuan; outstanding loan, 53 billion yuan; net profit, 1.08 billion yuan; capital adequacy ratio, 12.41%; Non-Performing Loan (NPL) Provisioning Coverage ratio, 534.45%; NPL ratio, 0.36%; the operating objective determined by the Board of Directors at the beginning of the year was reached, and the synchronized growth of the size, quality and benefits was materialized.

In 2011, the global economy will continue reviving and the context for economy will grow better; meanwhile, as the first year of “the Twelfth Five-year Plan” of PRC, 2011 will see the proactive fiscal policy and prudent monetary polity given by Chinese government to make economy normal. Therefore, accelerating the economy structure adjustment will be the new focus, which will introduce both opportunities and challenges to the Bank. This year, we will, “under the theme of scientific development and renovation”, adhere to the guiding principle of “preventing risk, adjusting structure, maintaining growth and promoting renovation” and serve the local economy, SMEs and citizens, to enhance our competitiveness, risk management and sustainable profitability so as to create more values for shareholders, customers and the whole society.

On behalf of the Board of Directors of the Bank, I would like to show my appreciation to those who are always supportive with us and extend my sincere gratitude to all our staff members. We will make further achievements and contributions this year!

Chairman: Ma Qianzhen

February 11, 2011

The Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, directors, supervisors and senior management members of Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd. undertake that the information stated in this report contains no fraudulent record, misleading statement or material omission, and assume joint and several liabilities as to the authenticity, accuracy and completeness of the content.

The Fourth Board of Directors of the Bank has in its third meeting held on February 11, 2011 reviewed and adopted this Annual Report. All the 15 directors attended the meeting. Seven supervisors of the Bank attended the meeting.

The financial statements of the Bank have been audited by Ascenda Certified Public Accountants, and standard unqualified audited reports thereon have been issued.

2. Cost-to-Income ratio = (Operating & Administrative expenses + Other operating expenses) / Operating income

##### 注：主要变动原因

1. 资本公积变动原因为可供出售金融资产公允价值变动；

2. 盈余公积、一般风险准备和未分配利润变动原因为净利润增加和进行利润分配。

##### Notes: Main Reasons:

1. Changes in capital reserve are caused by the changes in the fair value of the available-for-sale financial assets.

2. Changes in surplus reserve, general risk provision and undistributed profit are caused by the increase in net profit and distribution of profit.

##### 注：主要变动原因

1、表内应收利息，主要系新增交易性金融资产利息561万元、可供出售金融资产利息1849万元、持有至到期金融资产利息546万元、应收款项类投资利息600万元，以及收回了发放贷款和垫款利息27万元、买入返售金融资产利息110万元、存放同业款项利息187万元。

2、表外应收利息：主要系原逾期贷款产生的应收利息。

##### Notes: Main Reasons

1. The interest receivable in balance-sheet primarily comes from the increase interest in trading of financial assets 5.61 million yuan, the increase interest of available-for-sale financial assets 18.49 million yuan, the interest collection of held-to-maturity financial assets 5.46 million yuan, the interest collection of receivables 6 million yuan, the interest collection of loans and advances 270,000 yuan, the interest collection of reverse repurchase agreements 1.1 million yuan and the interest increase of no-trolliable account 1.87 million yuan.

2. The interest receivable in off-balance-sheet primary comes from interest receivables from previous overdue loans.

## Controlling Shareholders And De Facto Controller

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_155_379_1064_873.jpg" alt="Image" width="74%" /></div>


持有本行股权5%以上的股东情况：

Shareholders with over 5% shareholding:

# 1、重庆渝富资产经营管理有限公司

Chongqing Yufu Assets Management Co., Ltd

重庆渝富资产经营管理有限公司是成立于2004年2月27日的国有独资有限责任公司，出资人为重庆市人民政府，住所为重庆市渝中区人民路238号综合办公楼17楼，注册资本为10.2亿元，经营范围包括：市政府授权范围内的资产收购、处置及相关产业投资，投资咨询，财务顾问，企业重组兼并顾问及代理，企业和资产托管（国家法律法规规定须取得前置审批的，在未取得审批前不得经营）。

Chongqing Yufu Assets Management Co., Ltd. established on February 27, 2004, is a solely state-owned limited liability company and invested in by Chongqing Municipal People's Government. Registered address: 17F, Integrated Building, No. 238, Renmin Road, Yuzhong District, Chongqing; Registered capital: RMB 2.62 billion yuan; Business: asset acquisition & disposal and related industry investment authorized by the Government, investment consultation, financial consultation, enterprise re-organization and merger consultation and agency, enterprise trusteeship and asset custody (permit or approval must be obtained for those which require setup examination and approval as stipulated by national laws and regulations).

# 2、大新银行有限公司

Dah Sing Bank, Ltd.

大新银行有限公司1947年在香港注册成立，总部设在香港，为香港上市的大新银行集团全资持有之附属银行，大新银行集团之最终控股公司为于香港上市之大新金融集团。截至2010年12月31日，大新银行在香港拥有共计49家分行，在中国大陆通过其全资持有之国内法人银行大新银行(中国)拥有4家分行，另在澳门通过其全资持有之澳门商业银行另外拥有共15家分行。

Dah Sing Bank, Ltd. was registered and headquartered in Hong Kong in 1947. It is wholly held by Dah Sing Banking Group Limited, while Dah Sing Banking Group Limited is owned by Dah Sing Financial Holdings Limited which is listed in Hong Kong. As at December 31, 2010, Dah Sing Bank, Ltd. had owned 49 branches in Hong Kong. It also set up 4 branches in mainland China. Its subsidiary Banco Comercial De Macau had had 15 branches in Macau.

# 3、重庆路桥股份有限公司

Chongqing Road & Bridge Co., Ltd.

重庆路桥股份有限公司成立于1997年6月13日，是在上海证券交易所上市的股份有限公司，住所为重庆南坪经济技术开发区丹龙路11号，注册资本为4.54亿元，经营范围包括：重庆牛角沱大桥，长江石板坡大桥，嘉陵江石门大桥、南山旅游公路、自建资威公路资中段经营、维护、管理；市政公用工程施工总承包（壹级），房屋建筑工程（二级），房地产开发（二级）；销售建筑材料、装饰材料（不含危险化学品）、五金、金属材料（不含稀贵金属）、木材、建筑机械。

Chongqing Road & Bridge Co., Ltd., established on June 13, 1997, is listed in Shanghai Stock Exchange. Registered address: No. 11, Danlong Road, Nanping Economic & Technical Development Zone, Chongqing; Registered capital: RMB 454 million; Business: operation, maintenance and management of Niujiaotuo Bridge of Chongqing, Changjiang Shibanpo Bridge, Jialingjiang Shimen Bridge, Nanshan Tourism Highway and self-constructed Zizhong Section of Ziwei Highway; General Contract Grade A for Municipal Engineering Construction Works, Grade B for House & Building Construction, Grade B for Real Estate Development; sale of construction materials, decoration materials (excluding hazardous chemicals), hardware, metal (excluding precious metal), wood and construction machinery.

# 4、重庆市水利投资（集团）有限公司

Chongqing Water Conservancy Investment Group Co., Ltd.

重庆市水利投资（集团）有限公司是成立于2003年11月18日的国有独资有限责任公司，出资人为重庆市水利局，住所为重庆市渝北区龙溪街道新南路3号，注册资本为21.65亿元，经营范围包括：负责市政府授权范围内水利国有资产的营运管理，负责市级有关大中型水源的工程、供排水工程、治污工程的项目投资和经营，负责西部供水工程规划区内水资源的统一开发和经营，负责河道整治及水土保持项

目的开发、投资和经营，实施中小水电站项目开发，投资，销售水利水电设备及材料（国家法律法规有专项管理规定的除外）。

Chongqing Water Conservancy Investment Group Co., Ltd., established on November 18, 2003, is a wholly state-owned company and invested in by Chongqing Water Resources Bureau. Address: No. 3, Xinnan Road, Longxi Community, Yubei District, Chongqing; Registered capital: RMB 2.165 billion; Business: operation and management of state-owned water conservancy assets under the authorization of Chongqing Municipal People's Government; investment and operation of municipal large and medium water source projects, water supply/drainage projects and sewage projects; unified development and operation of water resources within the western water supply project plan; river regulation and development, investment and operation of water & land preservation projects; development and investment of medium and small hydropower stations; sale of water conservancy-related and hydroelectrical equipments and materials (except for those included in the special management by the national laws and regulations).

# 5、重庆市地产集团

Chongqing Land Properties Group

重庆市地产集团是成立于2003年4月4日的事业单位法人，实行企业化管理，举办单位为重庆市人民政府。住所为重庆市渝中区长江一路61号，注册资本20亿元，经营范围包括：为土地储备整治、土地整理和城镇廉租住房建设提供服务。建设用地的征用；土地收购储备和开发整治；耕地“占补平衡”；廉租住房项目建设；旧城改造建设；实施城市基础设施建设。

Chongqing Land Properties Group was established by Chongqing Municipal People's Government on April 4, 2003 as a legal representative for institutions. It applies enterprise-pattern management under the administration of Chongqing Municipal People's Government. Address: No. 61, First Changjiang Road, Yuzhong District, Chongqing; Registered capital: RMB 5 billion; Business: providing services for land preservation, improvement and arrangement as well as construction of urban subsidiary rental houses; expropriation of construction land; land acquisition, preservation, development and improvement; infield balance; construction of subsidiary rental house projects; urban reconstruction; construction of urban infrastructure.

# 6、力帆实业（集团）股份有限公司

Lifan Industry (Group) Co., Ltd.

力帆实业（集团）股份有限公司成立于1997年12月1日，于2010年11月在上海证券交易所上市，住所为重庆市沙坪坝区上桥张家湾60号，注册资本为9.51亿元，经营范围包括：研制、开发、生产、销售：车辆配件，摩托车配件，小型汽油机及配件，电动自行车及配件、汽油机助力车及配件，技术服务，计算机（不含研制），体育（限足球、摩托车运动）及运动产品（不含研制、生产），啤酒、含酒

精饮料、不含酒精饮料（不含研制、开发）；经营本企业研制开发的技术和生产的科技产品的出口业务；经营本企业科研和生产所需的技术、原辅材料、机械设备、仪器仪表、零配件的进口业务，经营本企业的进料加工和“三来一补”业务；经济信息咨询服务；批发、零售：家用电器、纸板及其制品、皮革制品、布料及纺织品、服装、鞋帽、玩具、雨伞、润滑油、润滑脂。

Lifan Industry (Group) Co., Ltd. was established on December 1, 1997, and listed in Shanghai Stock Exchange in November 2010. Address: No. 60, Zhangjiawan, Shangqiao, Shapingba District, Chongqing; Registered capital: 951 million; Business: research, development, production and sale of auto accessories, motorcycle accessories, small gasoline motor and accessories, electric bicycles and accessories, gasoline engine bikes and accessories, technical services, computers (excluding research & development), sporting products (limited to football and motorcycle products; excluding research & development), beer, alcohol drinks, non-alcohol drinks (excluding research & development); export of self-developed and produced technology and products; import of technology, raw/auxiliary material, machinery, instruments and components which are required for self-development and production; processing business with imported materials and processing and compensation trades (processing with materials or given samples, assembling supplied components); economic information consultation; wholesale and retail of household appliances, cardboard and related products, leather products, fabrics and textiles, garments, shoes & hats, tools, umbrellas, lubrication and grease.

## 4.1.7 董事、监事、高级管理人员的主要工作经历

Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management’s Profile

### 4.1.7.1 董事

Directors

### 马千真 执行董事/董事长/党委书记

Ma Qianzhen Executive Director/Chairman/ Secretary of Party Committee

高级管理人员工商管理硕士、高级会计师。1972年至1983年3月，在重庆铸造机械厂工作，其间：1974年9月至1976年8月在重庆师范专科学校中文系学习两年。1983年3月至2008年1月，历任重庆市财政局预算科办事员、科员，预算处副处长、处长，重庆市财政局副局长、局长、党组书记，其间：1997年5月至1999年5月在财政部科研所研究生班学习。2008年2月加入本行，其间：2009年5月至2010年12月在重庆大学经济与工商管理学院获高级管理人员工商管理硕士学位。现任本行第四届董事会董事长、党委书记；任中国银行业协会监事会监事。

Ma Qianzhen is the Executive Master of Business Administration background as senior accountant.

From 1972 to March 1983, Ms. Ma was working for Chongqing Casting Machinery Factory, and studying in the Department of Chinese Language and Literature in Chongqing Normal Specialized Postsecondary College from Sept. 1974 to Aug. 1976. From March 1983 to Jan. 2008, Ms. Ma had been successively serving as office worker and section member of the Budgeting Section, Division Chief of the Budgeting Division and Director & Party Leadership Group Secretary of Chongqing Finance Bureau. From May 1997 to May 1999, Ms. Ma was studying in the Research Institute for Fiscal Science, Ministry of Finance, PRC. Ma has been serving for the Bank since Feb. 2008 and was studying in the Economics & Business Administration in Chongqing University with EMBA obtained from May 2009 to Dec. 2010. Ms. Ma is currently Chairman of the Fourth Board of Directors and Secretary of Party Committee of the Bank as well as Supervisor of the Board of Supervisors of China Banking Association.

### 甘为民 执行董事、行长

##### Gan Weimin Executive Director/President

西南财经大学高级管理人员工商管理硕士，经济师。1989年7月参加工作，历任中国四川国际经济技术合作公司团支部书记，四川省嘉陵公司及嘉陵财务公司融资部经理，中信实业银行重庆分行公司业务部总经理、解放碑支行行长，中国民生银行重庆分行筹备组组长、行长、党委书记等。2006年12月加入本行，现任本行第四届董事会执行董事、行长；任中华全国青年联合会常务委员。

Gan Weimin, graduated from Southwestern University of Finance and Economics with the program of EMBA and obtained a master's degree and as economist. From July 1989, Mr. Gan served as League Branch Secretary of China Sichuan International Cooperation Co., Ltd., Manager of the Financing Department of Sichuan Jialing Co., Ltd. and Jialing Finance Co., Ltd., General Manager of Business Department of Chongqing Branch of CITIC Industrial Bank, Branch Manager of Jiefangbei Branch, Leader of Preparation Team of Chongqing Branch of China Minsheng Bank, President and Secretary of Party Committee of Chongqing Branch of China Minsheng Bank, etc. Since Dec. 2006, Gan has served for the Bank and is Executive Director of the Fourth Board of Directors and President of the Bank as well as the standing member of the All-China Youth Federation.

### 冉海陵 执行董事、副行长

##### Ran Hailing Executive Director/Vice President

高级管理人员工商管理硕士，经济师。1981年9月参加工作，历任涪陵地区行政公署办公室秘书、科长，涪陵地区罐头食品厂副厂长、党委委员，四川省信托投资公司涪陵办事处副总经理、党支部书记，四川省信托投资公司涪陵证券营业部总经理，西南证券有限责任公司总裁助理等。2003年3月加入本行，其间：2005年9月至2007年6月在重庆大学经济与工商管理学院获高级管理人员工商管理硕士学位，现任本行第四届董事会执行董事、副行长。

Ran Hailing is the Executive Master of Business Administration background as economist. Since Sept. 1981, Ran has successively served as Secretary and Section Chief of Fuling District Administrative Office, Vice Plant Manager and member of the Party Committee of Fuling Canned Food Plant, Vice General Manager and Party Branch Secretary of Fuling Office of Sichuan Trust and Investment Corporation, General Manager of Fuling Securities Business Department of Sichuan Trust and Investment Corporation

and Assistant to President of Southwest Securities Co., Ltd. From Sept. 2005 to June 2007, Ran was studying in the Economics & Business Administration in Chongqing University with EMBA obtained. Since March 2003, Mr. Ran has been serving for the Bank and is Executive Director of the Fourth Board of Directors and Vice President of the Bank.

#### 谷德荣 执行董事、副行长

##### Koo Tak Wing Executive Director/Vice President

美国西北大学及香港科技大学工商管理硕士，特许金融分析师、金融风险管理师。1991年起，历任美国大通银行信贷风险主任及副司库，日本债券信用银行高级企业业务主任，英国国民西敏银行高级信贷分析师，法国里昂信贷银行亚洲区银团贷款部副经理，恒生银行信贷风险经理、高级零售信贷风险经理、高级信贷经理、高级信贷系统经理，荷兰银行集团风险管理部副总裁等。2007年5月，经大新银行提名进入本行，现任本行第四届董事会执行董事、副行长。

Koo Tak Wing, graduated from Northwestern University (USA) and Hong Kong University of Science & Technology, with MBA and qualifications of Chartered Financial Analyst and Financial Risk Manager. Since 1991, Koo Tak Wing has served as Credit Risk Officer and Assistant Treasurer for Chase Manhattan Bank, Senior Business Deputy of Nippon Credit Bank, Senior Credit Analyst of British National Westminster Bank, Vice Manager of Regional Syndicated Loan Department of French Crédit Lyonnais, Credit Risk Manager, Senior Retail Credit Risk Manager, Senior Credit Manager, and Senior Credit System Manager of Hang Seng Bank, and Vice President of the Group Risk Management Department of ABN AMRO Bank. In May 2007, Mr. Koo was appointed by Dah Sing Bank to the Bank and is Executive Director of the Fourth Board of Directors and Vice President.

#### 黄汉兴 股东董事、副董事长

Derek H.H.Wong Director of Shareholder/Vice Chairman

毕业于香港理工大学。1977年加入大新银行，历任执行董事、行政副总裁，现任董事总经理兼行政总裁。同时，现任大新银行的母公司大新银行集团董事总经理兼行政总裁，大新金融集团有限公司董事总经理兼行政总裁，丰明银行有限公司执行董事，澳门商业银行有限公司董事，大新人寿保险有限公司董事会副主席，大新保险有限公司执行董事，长城人寿保险股份有限公司董事。现任本行第四届董事会副董事长。

Derek H.H.Wong, graduated from Hong Kong Polytechnic University. From 1977, Wong successively served as Executive Director and Executive Vice President of Dah Sing Bank. Derek Wong currently holds the position of Managing Director & Chief Executive of Dah Sing Bank. Derek Wong is also Managing Director & Executive President of Dah Sing Banking Group Limited, and of Dah Sing Financial Holdings Ltd., Executive Director of Mevas Bank Limited, Director of Banco Comercial De Macau, Vice Chairman of the Board & Executive Director of Dah Sing Life Assurance Company Ltd. and Director of Greatwall Life Insurance Co., Ltd. Derek Wong is currently Vice Chairman of the Fourth Board of Directors of the Bank.

### 尹明善 股东董事

Yin Mingsha Director of Shareholder

高中学历。1958年参加工作，历任重庆合成化工厂电大英语教师、重庆市广播电视大学英语中心教研组组长，重庆市设计院英语教师，重庆出版社编辑，重庆国际技术咨询公司总经理，重庆长江书刊公司董事长、重庆市工商联合会会长、重庆市第二届政协副主席等；1992年1月创建力帆集团，任力帆实业（集团）股份有限公司董事长、安诚财产保险股份有限公司董事、中国民间商会副会长、中国摩托车商会第二届名誉会长、本行第四届董事会股东董事。

Yin Mingshan, High School Diploma Holder. Since 1958, Mr. Yin had successively served as English teacher for Chongqing Synthetized Chemical Plant Open University, Teaching and Research Group Leader of English Center of Chongqing Radio and TV University, English teacher for Chongqing Architectural Design Institute, Editor for Chongqing Publishing Group, General Manager of Chongqing International Technology Consultant Co., Ltd., Chairman of the Board of Chongqing Changjiang Book Company, President of Chongqing Association of Industry & Commerce, Vice Chairman of the Second Chongqing People's Political Consultative Conference, etc. Mr. Yin established Lifan Group in January 1992 and has been Chairman of the Board since then. Besides, Mr. Yin is also Director of Ancheng Property & Casualty Insurance Co., Ltd., Vice President of All-China General Chamber of Industry & Commerce and President of the Second China Chamber of Commerce for Motorcycle. Mr. Yin is currently Director of Shareholder of the Fourth Board of Directors of the Bank.

Mr. Li is currently Secretary to the Board of Directors & Head of the Board of Directors' Office.

## 4.1.8 董事、监事、高级管理人员年薪情况

##### Annual Remuneration of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management

本公司董事、监事及高级管理人员的薪酬标准按股东大会审议通过的《重庆银行领导班子薪酬管理办法（试行）》和《股东董事、股东监事、独立董事、外部监事等津贴标准》执行。

The standard of remuneration for directors, supervisors and senior management should be in accordance with Remuneration Management Regulation for Leadership of Bank of Chongqing (trial) and Allowance Standard for Directors of Shareholder, Supervisors of Shareholder, Independent Directors and External Supervisors that have been approved by the Board of Directors.

2010年度，本公司执行董事、内部监事和高级管理人员共12人（含已离职的执行董事丁世录），预发薪酬（含五险一金，税前）共计764.41万元，待财政部和重庆市国资委公布有关考核数据后按照《重庆银行领导班子薪酬管理办法》进行清算。

In 2010, there were 12 executive directors, internal supervisors and senior management (including the resigned executive director Ding Shilu), and the total remuneration (including "five insurance & housing provident funds" before tax) was RMB 7,644,100, which will be cleared according to Remuneration Management Regulation for Leadership of Bank of Chongqing after the Ministry of Finance and Chongqing State-owned Assets Supervision and Administration Commission issuing related evaluation data.

##### 股东董事、股东监事、独立董事、外部监事15人，领取津贴（税前）共计75万元。

There were 15 shareholder-directors, shareholder-supervisors, independent directors and external supervisors, and the allowance paid (before tax) amounted to RMB 750,000.

# 4.2 报告期内董事、监事、高级管理人员变更情况

## Changes of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management during the Reporting Period

1、2010年5月21日，本行2009年年度股东大会审议批准童海洋先生因工作调动，辞去执行董事职务。同时，根据2009年年度股东大会决议和重庆银监局《关于丁世录任职资格的批复》（渝银监复〔2010〕64号），改选时任副行长兼财务负责人丁世录先生为执行董事。

1. Mr.Tong Haiyang is approved to be resigned from Executive Director of the Bank because of job change after 2009 Shareholders' General Meeting held on May 21, 2010, Meanwhile, in accordance with resolutions of 2009 Shareholders' General Meeting and Reply on the Qualification of Director Ding Shilu (Y. Y. J. F. (2010) No. 64) from CBRC (Chongqing Office), Mr. Ding Shilu, Vice President of the Bank and Chief Financial Officer, would serve as Executive Director of the Bank.

2、2010年12月9日，公司董事会接到丁世录先生的辞职报告，申请因工作调动，辞去本行执行董事、副行长、财务负责人职务，辞职报告自送达董事会时生效，且自动失去审计委员会、信息科技指导委员会委员职务。2010年12月29日召开的2010年第一次临时股东大会审议并同意了丁世录先生的辞职申请。

2. On December 9, 2010, the Board of Directors of the Bank received the resignation of Mr. Ding Shilu as Executive Director, Vice President & Chief Financial Officer of the Bank, which was with effect from the date of submission. Meanwhile, Mr. Ding Shilu was automatically disqualified as member of the Audit Committee and the Information Technology Steering Committee. At 2010 1st Extraordinary General Meeting convened on December 29, 2010, Mr. Ding's resignation was reviewed and approved.

3、2010年12月29日，本行2010年第一次临时股东大会审议并同意了董事会换届议案。由于第三届董事会董事任期届满，股东大会选举出第四届董事会董事，马千真、黄汉兴、甘为民、尹明善、吕维、谷德荣、吴家宏、肖昌华、范上钦等9名董事留任，新当选董事6名，分别是：执行董事冉海陵；股东董事刘良才、覃伟；独立董事孙芳城、张卫国、韩德云。上述6名新任董事的任职资格已于2011年2月18日经重庆银监局核准。

3. At 2010 1st Extraordinary General Meeting on December 29, 2010, the Bank reviewed and approved the Proposal for General Election of the Board of Directors. Due to the expiration of the tenure of the Third Board of Directors, Shareholders' General Meeting would elect the directors for the Fourth Board of Directors. There were 9 directors retaining the post, including Ma Qianzhen, Derek H.H. Wong, Gan Weimin, Yin Mingshan, Lv Wei, Koo Tak Wing, Wu Jiahong, Xiao Changhua and Horace S.Y.Fan. There were 6 directors appointed, including Executive Director Ran Hailing, Director of Shareholders Liu Liangcai and Qin Wei, and Independent Directors Sun Fangcheng, Zhang Weiguo and Han Deyun. The aforementioned 6 newly appointed directors were required to apply for qualification from banking regulatory agencies.

4、2010年12月4日、12月29日，本行分别召开第二届职工代表大会第一次会议和2010年第一次临时股东大会，审议并同意了监事会换届的相关议案。由于第三届监事会监事任期届满，经职工代表大会和股东大会选举，陈消、任诚、刘建华、陈建伟等4名监事留任，陈建伟由于工作调动原因，由股东监事变更为外部监事。新当选监事3名，分别是：股东监事司厚春、刘兴域，外部监事周永康。

4. On December 4, 2010 and December 29, 2010, the 1st Meeting of the 2nd Congress of Workers and Staff and 2010 1st Extraordinary General Meeting was respectively held. At the meetings, the Bank reviewed and approved the Proposals for General Election of the Board of Supervisors. Due to the expiration of the tenure of the Third Board of Supervisors, the Congress of Workers and Staff and the Shareholders' General Meeting would elect the supervisors for the Fourth Board of Supervisors. Chen Xiao, Ren Cheng, Liu Jianhua and Chen Jianwei remained being Supervisor, among who Chen Jianwei, owing to job changes, were appointed as External Supervisor instead of former position as Supervisor of Shareholder. Besides, 3 newly elected supervisors were Supervisor of Shareholder Si Houchun and Liu Xingyu as well as External Supervisor Zhou Yongkang.

# 5、2009年12月29日，根据甘为民行长提名，第四届董事会第一次会议审议批准倪月敏女士为本行财务负责人。

5. At the 1st Meeting of the Fourth Board of Directors held on December 29, 2009, according to the nomination of President Gan Weimin, Ms Ni Yuemin was proposed as Chief Financial Officer of the Bank. Ms. Ni would be officially appointed after the qualification from banking regulatory agencies was obtained.

# 4.3 员工情况

#### Employees

截至报告期末，公司在岗员工2,281人。按年龄结构分：30岁以下的占45.46%，31-40岁的占39.37%，41-50岁的占13.68%，51-60岁的占1.49%；按文化结构分，大学本科以上占68.3%，大专学历的占28.4%，中专学历及以下的占3.3%；按职称结构分，具有中高级职称的占17.54%，初级职称的占12.1%，公司需承担费用的离退休人员为166人。

At the end of the reporting period, the Bank had 2,281 employees. In terms of age structure, employees under 30 account for 45.46%, 31 to 40 account for 39.37%, 41 to 50 account for 13.68% and 51 to 60 account for 1.49%; in terms of education structure, bachelor's degree holders account for 68.3%, Junior Posts holders account for 28.4% and qualification below associate degree holders account for 3.3%; and in terms of professional title structure, employees of senior and middle management account for 17.54% and 12.1% respectively. And the Bank is responsible for the corresponding payment to total 166 retired staff.

### 第五节 公司治理结构

#### Corporate Governance Structure

# 5.1 本行公司治理情况

Corporate Governance

报告期内，本公司严格遵守《公司法》、《商业银行法》等规定，比照上市公司标准执行相关法律法规，并结合实际情况，不断完善公司治理结构、提升公司治理水平，规范公司运作。

In compliance with the stipulations of the Company Law of People's Republic of China, Law of the People's Republic of China on Commercial Banks and other relevant regulations, the Bank improved the structure of corporate governance, elevated the level of governance to a further extent and regulated the operation.

## 5.1.1 完善公司治理基础性制度

##### Improving Fundamental System of Corporate Governance

报告期内，公司根据最新监管要求和自身发展需要，不断完善公司治理的基础性制度建设。先后两次修订了《公司章程》，对提供担保、董事会各专委会职责、独立董事任期和固定资产交易等事项进行了修订和完善；为进一步强化信息科技风险管理，提高信息科技治理水平，成立了董事会信息科技指导委员会，制定了《信息科技指导委员会工作细则》；根据最新监管要求，制定了《声誉风险管理办法》和《流动性风险管理办法》；为提高薪酬管理的科学性，根据银监会《商业银行稳健薪酬监管指引》及财政部的相关规定，制定了《重庆银行领导班子薪酬管理办法（试行）》；为贯彻国家发展农村金融的战略部署，积极参与城乡统筹试验，制定了《关于设立村镇银行的指导意见（试行）》。此外，还根据宏观经济金融形势，调整制定了《2010年风险管理策略》、《2010年资产负债管理策略》，正着手在《重庆银行2007年—2011年战略规划》的基础上制定《重庆银行2011年—2015年战略规划》等。上述规章制度为本行进一步完善公司治理提供了基础性的制度保障。

During the reporting period, the Bank kept improving fundamental system of corporate governance. The Articles of Association was revised twice for modifying and consummating matters relating to guarantee offering, responsibilities of special committees under the Board of Directors, tenure of Independent Director and fixed assets transaction. The Information Technology Steering Committee under the Board of Directors was established and Detailed Guide for Information Technology Steering Committee was drawn up in order to enhance information and technology risk management and the level of information and technology management. Reputation Risk Management Regulation and Liquidity Risk Management Regulation were formulated according to latest supervisory requirements. Remuneration Management Regulation for Leadership of Bank of Chongqing (trial) was established according to Guidelines for Steady Remuneration of Commercial Banks from CBRC and other related regulations from the Ministry of Finance, so as to improve scientificity of remuneration management. The Bank also implemented national strategic plan of developing rural economy, actively took part in urban-rural overall test and formulated Guidelines for Establishing Banks in Villages and Small Towns (trial). Besides, the Bank, according to macro financial situation, adjusted and drew up 2010 Risk Management Strategy, 2010

Assets and Liabilities Management Strategy, and is now working on establishing 2011-2015 Strategic Plan for Bank of Chongqing on the basis of 2007-2011 Strategic Plan for Bank of Chongqing. Aforementioned regulations and policies lay a basic foundation for improving corporation governance.

## 5.1.2 关于股东与股东大会

## Shareholders and Shareholders' General Meeting

本公司在报告期内召开了2009年度股东大会和一次临时股东大会，各次会议的通知、召集、召开和表决程序均符合《公司法》和《公司章程》的规定。股东大会审议通过了董事会工作报告、监事会工作报告、董监事换届、修订公司章程、领导班子薪酬办法、财务预决算、利润分配、聘任会计师事务所、股东大会对董事会授权、购买营业用房等22项重大议案。

公司在报告期内召开的两次次股东大会均聘请了重庆伊斯特律师事务所对会议的合法、有效性进行现场见证，并出具了法律意见书。

During the reporting period, the Bank held 2009 Shareholders' General Meeting and an Extraordinary General Meeting. The procedure of announcement, convening, holding and voting were in conformity with the Company Law and Articles of Association. In the meeting, the Board of Directors reviewed and approved 22 important proposals relating to work report of the Board of Directors, work report of the Board of Supervisors, tenure of directors and supervisors, revision of Articles of Association, remuneration management regulation for leadership, budge and final accounts, profit distribution, accounting firm appointment, authorization from the General Meeting to the Board of Directors, and purchase of business occupancy.

The Bank also appointed Chongqing East Law Firm to witness all Shareholders' General Meetings held during the reporting period and the law firm issued legal opinion letter to confirm the validity of these meetings.

## 5.1.3 关于董事和董事会

Directors and Board of Directors

1、董事会构成及运作情况：

Structure of the Board of Directors

报告期内，本行第三届董事会由15名董事组成，其中包括5名独立董事；第四届董事会仍由15名董事组成，包括5名独立董事，董事会的人数和人员构成符合法律法规的要求。全体董事均能认真出席会议并审议各项议案，勤勉尽职，有效发挥决策职能，维护全体股东和公司整体利益。2010年，公司共召开董事会会议9次，听取报告37项，审议议案72项，内容包括经营计划、公司治理、董事换届、风险管理、财务审计、内部控制、制度建设、重大关联交易等事项。

During the reporting period, the Third Board of Directors consisted of 15 directors, among which 5 were independent directors; and the Fourth Board of Directors was still made up of 15 directors, among which 5 were independent directors. The number and the composition of the Board of Directors were in conformity with relevant laws and regulations. Directors attended and carefully reviewed and approved proposals to safeguard interests of shareholders and the Bank. The Bank held 9 board meetings, debriefed

37 reports and reviewed 72 proposals in 2010 which referred to operation planning, corporate governance, tenure of directors, risk management, financial audit, internal control, capital management and significant related party transactions, etc.

# 2、董事会各专门委员会运作情况：

Special Committees of the Board of Directors

本行董事会下设战略发展委员会、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、审计委员会、薪酬与提名委员会、信息科技指导委员会。各专门委员会的结构和人员构成均符合监管机构和《公司章程》的规定，各专委会认真研究公司的重要事项，尤其注重发挥独立董事的作用，以进一步提高董事会决策的科学性和决策效率。报告期内，六个专门委员会共计召开37次会议，听取、审议了117项议案，内容包括：经营计划、网点规划、预决算方案、机构设置、董事换届、重大关联交易、重大人事任免、领导班子薪酬、风险监测报告、内部审计报告、各类专项咨询报告、聘任会计师事务所等。六个专委会均能及时召开会议、认真审议议案，有效发挥了专门委员会的专业决策职能。

The Board of Directors of the Bank consisted of 6 special committees, i.e. the Strategic Development Committee, the Related Party Transactions Control Committee, the Risk Control Committee, the Audit Committee, the Remuneration and Nomination Committee and the Information Technology Steering Committee. The number and the composition of the committees were in conformity with the Articles of Association and requirements of regulators. The 6 special committees carefully reviewed significant issues and paid attention to play the full function of independent directors, so as to enhance the scientificity and efficiency of decisions made by the Board of Directors. All these committees held 37 meetings, debriefed and reviewed 117 proposals during the reporting period, giving professional decision-making function into full play. Proposals being reviewed and approved included business plan, branch establishment plan, budget/final accounts plan, organization structuring, tenure of directors, significant related party transactions, important staff nomination and dismiss, leadership remuneration, risk monitoring report, internal audit report, specific consultant report, appointment of certified public accountants, etc.

根据本行2010年12月29日第四届董事会第一次会议审议通过的《关于选举重庆银行股份有限公司第四届董事会专门委员会成员的议案》及第四届董事会各专门委员会第一次会议审议通过的相关议案，第四届董事会各专门委员会委员及秘书具体组成如下（按姓氏笔划排名）：

# 3、董事开展调研及对外交流、学习情况

Investigation and External Exchange by Directors

报告期内，董事开展了以下调研和对外交流、学习活动

During the reporting period, the Board of Directors carried out a series of surveys, external communications and activities:

（1）2010年4月，副董事长黄汉兴，董事何智亚、顾玫、陈辉明、肖昌华、谷德荣，董事会秘书李在宁及相关人员赴西安实地考察了西安分行选址情况，积极促进本行跨区域发展。

（2）2010年6月，为进一步加强与城商行及业务合作单位的联系和沟通，学习其先进发展经验并深入了解合作单位情况，由陈消监事长带队，本行部分执行董事、独立董事和外部监事赴上海银行和中国银联数据中心进行了交流、学习。

（3）2010年7月，马千真董事长、原执行董事丁世录、李在宁董秘参加了由重庆市上市公司协会组织的“2010年第一期上市公司、拟上市公司董监高培训班”，学习了宏观经济形势对资本市场及实体经济的影响、上市公司内幕交易及其监管等课程。

（4）2010年8月，由马千真董事长、甘为民行长、陈消监事长分别带队，本行执行董事、副行长、相关部室负责人共三批次分赴宁波银行、北京银行、大连银行进行考察，就三家城商行在跨区域经营下的组织架构设计及管理模式进行了学习、交流。

（5）2010年8月，为提升履职能力，本行新任董事吕维参加了中国银监会监管二部组织的2010年第二期城商行董事培训班，学习了最新的监管法规、商业银行公司治理运作等内容。

(1) In April, 2010, Vice Chairman Derek H.H. Wong, Director He Zhiya, Director Gu Mei, Director Chen Huiming, Director Xiao Changhua, Director Koo Tak Wing, Secretary Li Zaining and other related staff carried out an investigation for site of Xi'an Branch, which actively expedited the cross-regional development of the Bank.

(2) In June 2010, in order to further strengthen the connection and communication with urban commercial banks and business cooperating units, to learn more about their advanced experience in development and to get an in-depth knowledge of the business cooperating units, Chief Supervisor Chen Xiao led some executive directors, independent directors and external supervisors of the Bank to communicate and share experience in the Bank of Shanghai and the Data Center of CUP.

(3) In July 2010, Chairman Ma Qianzhen, Former Executive Director Ding Shilu, and Secretary Li Zaining attended "2010 1st Advanced Training Course of Directors and Supervisors of Listed Company and Pre-IPO Company" organized by Chongqing Listed Company Association, learning the influences of macroeconomic situation on capital market and substantial economy, insider dealing and its supervision of listed companies, etc.

(4) In August, 2010, respectively led by Chairman Ma Qianzhen, President Gan Weimin and Chief Supervisor Chen Xiao, executive directors, vice presidents and chiefs of related sections left for Bank of Ningbo, Bank of Beijing and Bank of Dalian to investigate and communicate on organizational structure and management mode under the situation of cross-regional operation.

(5) In August, 2010, with the purpose of enhancing capacity of performing duties, newly-appointed Director Lv Wei attended "2010 2nd Training Course of Directors of Urban Commercial Banks" organized by Supervision Section II of CBRC, studying latest laws and regulations on supervision, governance and operation of commercial banks, etc.

## 5.1.4 关于监事、监事会

### Supervisors and the Board of Supervisors

报告期内，本公司第三届和第四届监事会均由7名监事组成，其中包括2名外部监事。监事会的人数和人员构成符合法律法规的要求。2010年，监事会共召开9次会议，审议议案41项，监事及监事代理人列席了本年度召开的全部董事会和股东大会。监事认真审议各项议案，独立发表意见，体现了较强的履职能力和责任心，有效实施监督职能，维护了全体股东及本公司的合法权益。

本公司监事会下设监督及提名委员会，由1名职工监事，2名外部监事构成。能够按照法律法规、公司章程、议事规则的要求召开会议，报告期内，共召开6次监督及提名委员会会议，主要对第四届监事候选人进行资格审查，组织实施了14位董事的履职评价、1位高级管理层成员的离任审计、2次专项检查及2次专项调研。

During the reporting period, the Third and Fourth Boards of Supervisors both consisted of 7 supervisors among which 2 were external supervisors. The number and the composition of the Board of Supervisors were in conformity with relevant laws and regulations. The Board of Supervisors held

9 meetings and reviewed 41 proposals. Supervisors or their agents attended the meetings of the Board of Directors and shareholders' meetings. The supervisors seriously reviewed all the proposals and gave independent opinions, reflecting their strong performance capability and responsibility. The Board of Supervisors conducted effective supervision, which guaranteed the legal rights of the Bank and the shareholders.

The Board of Supervisors administered the Supervision & Nomination Committee which was able to hold meetings in accordance with laws, articles of association and rules of procedure. During the reporting period, the Committee held 6 meetings, referring to qualification examination of candidates of the Fourth Board of Supervisors, performance assessment on 14 supervisors, departure audit for 1 senior manager and 2 special inspections.

## 5.1.5 关于信息披露和投资者关系

#### Information Disclosure and Investor Relations

报告期内，本行作为公众公司和拟上市公司，按照中国银监会的要求，并比照中国证监会及交易所的相关规定，着力提高信息披露质量和日常管理水平。本行履行信息披露义务，主要采取报刊、互联网等媒介进行发布，确保信息披露的及时、准确、真实、完整。2010年，准确、及时披露了《2009年年度报告》和16份临时公告。

公司注重与投资者的沟通交流，不断完善与投资者的沟通机制，注重加强与投资者的双向沟通，由董事会办公室接待股东的来电、来信、来访和咨询，在公司网站优化了“投资者关系”专栏，及时提供本行的经营动态和最新信息，便于股东了解最新情况。在日常工作中，积极与投资者保持联系，增进投资者对公司的了解和信任，树立本行诚信、规范、稳健的良好市场形象。

As a public company and a pre-IPO company, the Bank puts great effort on advancing information disclosure quality and operation in accordance with the requirements of CBRC, CSRC and stock exchange. Meanwhile, the Bank complied with the requirements of information disclosure and disclosed significant events on newspapers, journals and internet, which guaranteed the authentication, timeliness, accuracy and completeness of the information. In 2010, the Bank disclosed 2009 Annual Report and 16 temporary announcements accurately and timely.

The Bank emphasized communication with investors, perfected the communication mechanism and implemented the two-way communication. The Office of the Board of Directors made proper response to calls, letters, visits and consultation from shareholders, and opened up a special column ("Investor Relations") on the Bank's website to provide the operating status and latest information of the Bank as well as facilitate shareholders' understanding of the information about the Bank. The Bank also communicated frequently with investors to cultivate mutual understanding and trust as well as establish a positive image of reliability, stability and honesty.

# 5.2 独立董事履职情况

Responsibilities and Duties of Independent Directors

## 5.2.1 独立董事总体工作情况

Responsibilities of Independent Directors

董事会现有独立董事5名，由具备金融、法律、会计、审计等专业知识的社会人士担任。独立董事人数和人员构成符合相关法律法规的要求。

报告期内，独立董事严格按照相关法律法规以及本公司章程、独立董事制度和各专委会工作细则的要求，认真履行职责，参与公司重大决策，对定期报告、关联交易、人事任免、利润分配等重大事项发表了独立客观的意见，切实维护公司整体利益，尤其是注重保护中小股东的合法权益。

The Board of Directors now includes 5 independent directors who are social individuals with professional knowledge of finance, law, accounting, audit, etc. The number and the composition of independent directors are in conformity with relevant laws and regulations.

During the reporting period, independent directors seriously carried out responsibilities according to relevant laws and regulations, the articles of association, regulations for independent directors and detailed operation regulation for special committees. Meanwhile, the independent directors also actively participated in the significant decision making of the Bank, provided opinions and special statements on various significant issues, such as periodic reports, related-party transactions, appointment and dismiss and profit distribution, practically maintaining the overall interests of the Bank, especially legal rights and interests of the small and medium shareholders.
