## 5.2.3 独立董事对公司有关事项提出异议情况

Objection Brought up by Independent Directors in Relation to Issues Involved with the Bank

报告期内，独立董事未对公司董事会议案和其他非董事会议案提出反对意见。

During the reporting period, independent directors did not bring up any objection regarding proposals approved by the Board of Directors or others.

# 5.3 本行经营决策体系 Decision-making System of the Bank

本行最高权力机构为股东大会，董事会、监事会对股东大会负责，按照公司章程的规定分别履行决策、管理和监督职能。行长受聘于董事会，对本行的日常经营管理全面负责。本行实行一级法人体制，各分支机构均为非独立核算单位，其经营管理活动根据总行授权进行，并对总行负责。

本行无控股股东和实际控制人。本行与大股东在业务、人员、资产、机构、财务等五个方面完全独立。本行为自主经营、自负盈亏的独立法人，具有独立、完整的业务和自主经营能力。

Shareholders’ General Meeting is the power institution of the Bank. The Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors are responsible for the Shareholders’ General Meeting. According to the Articles of Association, these three institutions carried out functions of implementation, management and supervision respectively. The President shall be appointed by the Board of Directors and responsible for the management of daily operations. The Bank adopts the “single-legal person system” with every branch or sub-branch as non-independent accounting unit whose operation and management activities should be based on the authorization of the head office and who should be responsible for the head office.

The Bank does not have controlling shareholder or de facto controller. The Bank is completely independent in five aspects of business, staff, asset, organization and finance. The Bank is an independent legal entity and shall be only responsible for its own operation and profits or losses. The Bank has independent and complete businesses and has the ability to make its own management decisions.

# 5.4 高级管理层成员考核、激励与约束机制 Assessment, Incentive and Restraint System of the Senior Management

报告期内，根据中国银监会《商业银行稳健薪酬监管指引》、财政部《关于金融类国有及国有控股企业绩效评价暂行办法》和《金融类国有及国有控股企业绩效评价实施细则》的规定，并比照财政部《中央金融企业负责人薪酬审核管理办法》，制定了《重庆银行领导班子薪酬管理办法（试行）》，董事会、股东大会审议通过了该办法，并据此对高级管理层进行认真考核，定期开展了高管人员的述职评价。

During the reporting period, according to Guidelines for Steady Remuneration of Commercial Banks from CBRC and Rules for Implementing Remuneration Examination and Performance Evaluation of Leadership of Financial Enterprise under Central Government from the Ministry of Finance, and in comparison with Remuneration Management Regulation for Leadership of Financial Enterprise under Central Government from the Ministry of Finance, the Bank worked out Remuneration Management Regulation for Leadership of Bank of Chongqing (trial). The Board of Directors and the General Meeting of Shareholders reviewed and approved the aforementioned trial regulation, carried out the assessment of the senior managers and tried out the performance report assessment for senior management.

# 5.5 公司内部控制建立健全情况

Establishment & Improvement of Internal Control System

## 5.5.1 内部控制建设的总体方案

General Proposal on the Establishment of Internal Control System

董事会是本行建立并实施充分有效的内部控制体系的决策机构，负责保证高级管理层对内部控制体系的充分性与有效性进行监测和评估；监事会是本行负责监督董事会、高级管理层完善内部控制体系，承担监督董事会及董事、高级管理层及高级管理人员履行内部控制的职责；高级管理层负责制定内部控制政策，对内部控制体系的充分性与有效性进行监测和评估，负责执行董事会决策，负责建立和完善内部组织机构，保证内部控制的各项职责得到有效履行。董事会审计委员会是按照股东大会决议设立的专门工作机构，主要负责本行内、外部审计的沟通、监督和核查工作。

本行以构建完善的公司治理结构和特有的内部控制文化为基础，以健全的内部控制制度和严密的控制措施为核心，以各营业机构的自律检查、各业务条线的检查辅导和稽核监督评价体系为手段，以计算机信息系统和通畅的沟通交流渠道为依托的内部控制体系。董事会将持续推动内部控制建设，促进内控体系不断健全和完善，追求长期、持续、稳健的经营和发展。

根据法律法规和监管要求，本行内部控制渗透或涵盖银行的各项业务过程和各个操作环节，覆盖所有的部门和岗位，并与本行的经营规模、业务范围和风险特点基本相适应。

The Board of Directors of the Bank took the responsibility for the establishment and implementation of a sufficient and effective internal control system, and guaranteed the inspection and evaluation on the sufficiency and effectiveness of the internal control system by approval authorities and the senior management of the Bank. The Board of Supervisors was responsible for supervising the Board of Directors and the senior management to perfect the internal control system, and also supervising the Board of Directors, directors, the senior management and senior managers in relation to their performance of internal control duties. The senior management was responsible for the formation of internal control policies, supervision and evaluation on the sufficiency and effectiveness of internal control system, implementation of decisions made by the Board of Directors, setup and improvement of internal organization structure and the effective performance of all internal control related duties.

The Bank basically set up an internal control system which was grounded on the perfect banking governance structure and typical internal control culture and emphasized on improved internal control system and strict control measures. This system also adopted self-check by each business organization, examination and supervision on each business as well as audit and supervision mechanism, and relied on the computer information system and smooth communication channels. The Board of Directors would further the setup of internal control to ensure the continuous improvement of internal control system and the stable and persistent development of the Bank.

According to laws, regulations and supervisory requirements, the internal control of the Bank infiltrated or covered all business processes and every procedure, referred to all departments and positions, and basically corresponded to business scale, business scope and risk characteristics of the Bank.

报告期内，内部控制建设重点工作实施情况如下：

During the reporting period, the focuses of internal control were as follows:

一是持续推进内控“三道防线”的建立和完善。初步建立了分支行自查自纠，总行业务管理部门、风险管理部门常态化滚动式检查和辅导，稽核再监督检查三个不同层面的监督纠正管理机制，形成了检

查、整改、后续监督纠正闭环操作，确保各项业务的持续改进和优化，监督成效较明显。

二是聘请咨询公司对我行信息科技管理现状参照指引要求和行业最佳实践进行了差距评估分析，根据评估结果，我行已制定了全面的整改计划，目前正在按计划整改。

三是合规风险管理系统正式上线运行，建立了全行合规管理的信息化平台。通过该项目的实施，实现了业务规章制度的系统化、流程化、规范化，初步建立了适合于我行的合规评估体系。

四是以风险为导向，逐步探索内审工作方式和方法的改进。本年通过对会计业务流程、信贷业务流程以及综合管理、财务管理环节风险点梳理工作，运用风险导向审计技术，初步建立了信贷流程、会计结算流程、财务和综合管理的风险导向审计模型，并将风险导向稽核模型应用于今年的现场稽核检查，取得了明显效果，逐步提升了稽核效能，发挥了风险监控的功能。

First, carry forward the establishment and perfection of "three preventive measures"; set up three levels of supervision and correction management mechanism referring to self-check and self-correction by branches and sub-branches, routine roll-check and guideline by business management department and risk management department, as well as supervision and examination after audit; carried out "closed loop" operation in relation to examination, rectification, follow-up supervision and correction to ensure the continuous improvement and optimization of businesses with apparent results.

Second, appoint consulting company to perform a difference evaluation analysis on Info-tech management status quo on the basis of reference guidelines and best industrial practice. According to the result of analysis, the Bank had made up a complete corrective plan and is now implementing it.

Third, formally start to run compliance risk management system and set up an information platform for compliance risk management of the Bank. By implementing the project, the Bank had achieved systemization, routing and standardization of business rules and regulations, and initially built a compliance risk evaluation system which was suitable for the Bank.

Fourth, oriented by risks, gradually seek improving methods for internal inspection. In 2010, the Bank had applied risk-oriented audit technique in coping with. Therefore, risk-oriented audit mode had been initially set up in accounting business process, credit business process as well comprehensive management and financial management. Besides, risk-oriented audit mode was used in on-site audit and examination. It not only achieved good result, but also gradually increased audit proficiency and played full function of risk monitoring.

#### 5.5.2 公司内控制度建立情况

##### Establishment of Internal Control System

本行制定了《内部控制制度管理办法》，报告年度又根据管理需要进行了修订与补充，进一步规范内部控制制度的制定程序，明确了内部控制制度制定的要求与责任，提升了内控管理水平。同时，通过加强培训，强化检查监督，持续提高制度执行力。

报告年度，已按照计划制定或修订内控制度共计52个，涉及授信业务、资金业务、存款与柜台业务、中间业务、电子银行业务、财务管理、计算机信息等，如：《重庆银行中小企业授信业务管理办法（2010年版）》、《重庆银行个人汽车消费贷款管理办法》、《重庆银行债券结算代理业务管理办法》、《重庆银行反洗钱管理办法（修订）》、《重庆银行客户洗钱风险等级分类管理办法》、《自助设备管理办法》、《重庆银行呆账核销管理办法》、《流动性管理办法》、《重庆银行IT软件开发项目绩效评估办法（2010年版）》等，进一步规范和加强了经营管理。

The Bank had formulated Management Regulations for Internal Control System, which was revised and supplied in Annual Report according to the actual management demands. It further standardized

the formulating procedures of the internal control system, specified the requirements and responsibilities contained in the internal control system, and thus improved the management level of internal control. Meanwhile, it was essential to enhance the executive power of the regulations by enforcing training, inspection and supervision

In reporting year, the Bank issued or revised 52 internal control regulations in respect of credit business, capital business, deposit and counter business and accounting management, intermediary business, international business, planning finance, security guard and information technology, such as Management Regulations for Credit Business of Medium and Small-sized Enterprise (2010 Edition), Management Regulation for Individual Auto Consumption Credit, Management Regulations for Bond Settlement Agent Service, Management Regulations for Anti-money Laundering (Revision), Management Regulations for Risk Classification of Customer Money Laundering, Management Regulations for Self-service Devices, Management Regulations for Verification & Cancellation of Bad Debts, Management Regulations for Liquidity, Evaluation Regulations for Performance of IT Software Developing Project (2010 Edition), etc.

## 5.5.3 公司内控检查监督机制运行情况

### Operation of Examination & Supervision Mechanism for Internal Control

本行指定稽核部负责组织检查、评价内部控制的健全性和有效性，督促业务部门和分支机构及时纠正内部控制存在的问题。

现已建立起垂直独立的内部审计机构—稽核部，稽核部对董事会审计委员会负责，实行向审计委员会和行长的双线报告制度。以改善运营、增加价值为宗旨，通过系统化、规范化的方法评价，协助改进银行经营活动、内部控制、风险管理及公司治理，促进组织目标的实现。为适应本行三级管理构架下的稽核监督机制的有效运行，2010年在跨区域分行设立了稽核监督机构，风险管控能力得到增强。

本行已初步建立了内部控制责任制。董事会、高管层对内部控制的有效性负责。稽核部对未执行审计方案、程序和方法导致重大问题未能被发现负责；对审计发现隐瞒或者未如实反映，审计结论与事实严重不符，审计发现问题查处整改跟踪不力负责。业务部门和分支机构对未及时纠正内部控制存在的问题，对出现的风险和损失负责。高管层对违反内部控制的人员，依据法律法规、本行制度进行问责追究。

The Bank appointed the Audit Department to be responsible for examining and evaluating the completeness and effectiveness of internal control as well as supervising business department and branches/sub-branches to timely correct problems of internal control.

The Bank has set up a vertically independent internal audit institution—Audit Department which is responsible to the Audit Committee of the Board of Directors, and reports to both the Audit Committee and the President. With the principle of improving operation and increasing value, the Audit Department conducted evaluation on and assisted in improving operation activities, internal control, risk management and corporate governance by means of systematic and standard approaches to realize organization objectives. In order to adapt effective operation of audit and supervision system under 3-level management structure, the Bank established audit and supervision section in cross-regional branches in 2010, thus enhancing the capability in managing risks.

The Bank has preliminarily established the responsibility system for internal control. The Board of Directors and the senior management are responsible for the effectiveness of internal control; the Audit Department is responsible for audit proposals or procedures or measures which are not carried out and lead

to undiscovered significant problems, any disguised or hidden audit events, any remarkable inconsistency between audit results and actual events and also failure in tracking the rectification to problems found during audit; the business departments and branches/sub-branches are responsible for existing internal control problems which are not solved timely or existing risks and losses; the senior management is responsible for calling employees who violate internal control system to account in accordance with laws and regulations of the Bank.

## 5.5.4 审计委员会履职情况

#### Responsibilities and Duties of the Audit Committee

报告年度，董事会审计委员会全体成员认真开展工作，较好地完成了公司章程及董事会赋予的各项工作任务。全年共召开会议6次，其中现场会议4次，传签会议2次，共计审议23项议案，均获得通过。其履职情况如下：

During the reporting year, all members of the Audit Committee made concerted efforts in their work and excellently finished tasks assigned by Articles of Association and the Board of Directors. The Audit Committee held 6 meetings including 4 on-the-spot meetings and 2 meetings by circular signature as well as discussed and approved 23 proposals. It fulfilled following duties:

一是在审计委员会第17次会议审议《关于继续聘请天健正信会计师事务所为我行审计师及增加审计服务内容的议案》时，要求会计师事务所向我行董事会提供《管理建议书》，同时，指出会计师事务所出具的《内控鉴证报告》应该对内控有很好的参考意见，该提议得到了积极响应。

1. When reviewing Proposal for the Continued Contract with Ascenda Certified Public Accountants as the Bank's 2010 Auditor and Increasing Audit Service Items at 17th Meeting of the Audit Committee, the Audit Committee required accounting firm to provide Management Proposals. Meanwhile, the Audit Committee pointed out good reference suggestions on internal control in Internal Control Authentication Report, which gained active response.

二是在第19次审计委员会会议上，委员们在审议《关于重庆银行2007年至2009年及2010年上半年财务报表及附注的议案》时，对本行政府平台贷款的情况给予了高度关注，详细了解了天健正信会计师事务所是否就此有专门的政府平台的报告、政府平台检查情况的反馈渠道等。委员会成员还向稽核部详细征询了本行钢铁、水泥、造船等6加1行业贷款排查的效果，并提出在向董事会报送的议案中，增加相应内容。对于稽核检查中一些具体检查出的问题，要求在相关报告中要有具体的解决措施。

2. When reviewing Proposal of Bank of Chongqing 2007-2009 & 2010 Interim Financial Statements and its Annotations at the 19th Meeting, the Audit Committee gave a lot of attentions on loans on the government platform and got an in-depth knowledge of whether Ascenda Certified Public Accountants had specific government platform report, feedback of government platform inspection, etc. Members also carefully consulted the Audit Department for the effects of 6+1 industry loan audit in steel, cement, ship manufacturing and other industries, and suggested that the related contents and information should be added in the proposals submitted to the Board of Directors. As for problems found in audit, detailed solutions or methods should be made in the related reports.

三是委员会成员高度关注本行内审工作和内审架构的完善。审计委员会通过决议，责成董事会办公室牵头计财部、稽核部通过议标方式，聘请毕马威对内审实施情况和组织架构进行了分析评介。依据毕马威的评介报告，提出了改进的意见，明确了逐步由一元化体系向二元化内审体系过渡的改革方向。审

计委员会还对稽核部落实《重庆银行2009年内审工作咨询报告》中的相关内容提出了时间性要求。要求稽核部做好员工分工，引进专业人员，尽可能采用先进的技术方法和手段，切实开展以风险为导向的各项内审工作，必要时可以借助外部力量来努力完善该项工作。

3. Members of the Audit Committee paid a lot of attentions on perfection of implementation and structure of internal audit. By resolution, the Audit Committee instructed the Executive Office of the Board of Directors to take the lead, requiring Financial Department and Audit Department to appoint KPMG to give an analytic evaluation on implementation and organizational structure of internal audit. According to evaluation report from KPMG, the Audit Committee put forward some suggestions for improvement, and specified the reform direction of transiting centralization system to dualization system of internal audit. Besides, time requirements were made on the Audit Department's implementation of Bang of Chongqing 2009 Internal Audit Consulting Report, requiring the Audit Department to reasonably assign tasks, introduce technicians, apply advanced technologies and approaches, and practically carried out risk-oriented internal audits. If necessary, external force could be used to perfect the audit work.

#### 5.5.5 内部控制自我评价工作开展情况

##### Implementation of Internal Control Self-assessment

按照全面、统一、独立、公正、重要、及时的原则，依据《企业内部控制基本规范》、《商业银行内部控制指引》、《商业银行内部控制评价试行办法》等要求，本行从内部控制环境、风险识别与评估、内部控制措施、信息交流与反馈、监督评价与纠正等方面，进行了自我评价，并形成了《关于内部控制制度完整性、合理性及有效性的评价报告》。同时，按照2010年度内审工作计划，对所辖独立核算主体支行的内部控制情况进行了自我评价。

本行根据实际建立了能满足管理需要的内部控制制度，能结合发展需要不断改进和提高，相关制度覆盖了业务活动和内部管理的主要方面和环节，在规范财务会计行为、提高会计信息质量、强化经营管理、控制经营风险、堵塞漏洞、防止舞弊等方面得到了较为有效地执行。本行目前的内部控制制度从组织体系、规章制度安排、实际实施力度和反馈效果来看是基本完整合理的，整体运行是有效的，不存在重大缺陷。

In the principle of comprehensiveness, uniformity, independence, equality, importance and timeliness as well as according to the requirements of "Basic Regulations on Corporate Internal Control", "Internal Control Guideline for Commercial Banks" and "Trial Measures of Internal Control Evaluation of Commercial Banks", the Bank conducted self-assessment in terms of internal control environment, risk identification and evaluation, internal control measures, information exchange and feedback, supervision, evaluation and correction. It formulated Assessment Report on the Completeness, Reasonability and Effectiveness of Internal Control System. Meanwhile, the Bank carried out self-assessment on the internal control of independent checking bodies under the administration in line with the internal audit plan of 2010.

The internal control system established on the basis of actual situation by the Bank is able to generally satisfy management demands and will be adjusted and improved by the Bank if necessary for development. The related systems cover primary aspects and steps of business activities and internal management, which are effectively implemented in relation to standardizing accounting behaviors, improving quality of accounting information, enhancing operating management, controlling operating risks, preventing mistakes and frauds. The internal control system of the Bank appears to be generally complete and rational in respect of organizational system, regulation arrangement, actual enforcement and feedback effects with efficient operation and without significant defects.

## 5.5.6 内部控制存在的缺陷及整改情况

Existing Defects of Internal Control System and Rectification

本行内部控制在信息技术、人力资源、风险管理、监督纠正等方面还存在如下薄弱环节：IT管理制度仍然需要继续完善；IT架构以及中心机房等基础设施建设相对滞后，有可能影响业务快速发展和跨区域经营的需要；随着业务的发展，对专业人才的需求日益加大；风险识别与评估体系需要加大技术含量，以适应监管及经营的需要；如何平衡风险控制与业务发展之间的关系，提高风险经营能力仍是风险管理工作中的重点和难点。

对于上述薄弱环节，本行已高度关注并采取相应措施尽可能予以解决。

The weakness of internal control system of the Bank is weak in information technology, human resources, risk management and supervision & correction: the IT information system and management mechanism shall be further improved; infrastructures like IT structure and center service room were comparatively hysteretic, which may impact rapid development or can not afford the demands of cross-regional operation; large demands of technicians and professionals emerging during the rapid development of businesses; the risk management system shall be strengthened and the risk evaluation work for operating departments shall be enhanced; how to balance the relationship between risk management and business development as well as how to enhance capability of risk operation were still the hard and difficult part of the whole risk management.

The Bank has taken measures to overcome the weakness by means of following improvement:

（一）按照银监会《银行业金融信息系统风险管理指引》的要求，加强信息系统建设，完善现有信息系统管理制度。目前我行已聘请了咨询公司对信息科技管理现状参照指引要求和行业最佳实践进行了差距评估分析，根据评估结果，正在落实对发现问题的整改。

（二）根据IT战略规划，我行正从基础架构、IT治理等方面进行落地实施，目前正在全力实施企业服务总线、IT服务管理系统的建设，完善项目管理与项目质量管理体系，进一步优化IT基础架构，同时正在规范IT运维操作流程，提高IT服务水平，提升监控手段，增强业务连续性保障。加快基础设施的建设，以满足业务发展的需求。

（三）构建和完善人力资源管理体系。主要包括组织管理体系、员工职业发展体系、激励约束体系、人才评价体系、人才培养体系、劳动关系管理体系等六大体系。在此基础上，完善各项管理制度，将体系内容贯彻到具体工作中，不断深化人力资源改革。

（四）合理有序地疏通进入渠道，引进满足业务发展和加强内控管理需要的人才；继续完善多元化的员工职业发展通道，将员工职级评定和聘任作为激发员工积极性的有效手段，疏通各类员工职业生涯发展路径。

（五）加强风险管理。不断健全适应审慎监管要求和业务发展需要的风险识别与评估体系，加强各类风险管理技术与方法的研究和运用，加强IT风险管理团队建设，进一步提升风险管理水平。

（六）牢固树立价值贡献理念和效率意识，进一步提高识别一般性合规问题与关键风险点的能力，在加强合规管理的同时，切忌矫枉过正，放大风险，阻碍业务发展，力争在监管要求、合规管理与业务发展之间寻求最佳平衡点。

（七）进一步促进操作风险防控与案防工作的结合。针对授信业务、会计结算与柜台业务、票据业务、重要岗位人员管理等8大重点业务和57个重点业务环节，仔细查找存在的问题，深入分析问题的根源。系统梳理并完善内控制度、业务操作流程，对具体问题制定整改推进措施，加大整治督导力度和操作风险防控力度，切实防止案件的发生。

(1) In according with the requirements of Guidelines on Risk Management of Information System for Banking Financial Institutions, the Bank strengthens the construction of information system and perfects the current information system management mechanism. Advisory company has been appointed to conduct evaluation and analysis on the differences between the current information technology management of the Bank with the referential guideline requirements as well as the Bank and the industry best practice awarder. Based on the evaluation results, corresponding adjustment work will be carried out.

(2) The Bank is implementing the IT strategy plan starting from basic structure and IT governance, focusing on the establishment of corporate service lines and IT service management system currently. Through these projects, the Bank will further optimize the IT basic structure and realize the uniform service standards, laying foundation on the reserve system. Meanwhile, the Bank standardizes the IT operation procedures, elevates IT service level, diversifies monitor approaches and increases guarantee for business continuity. The Bank also boosted infrastructure construction in order to meet the demands of business development.

(3) The Bank constructs and perfects human resources management system, mainly including organizational management system, employer vocational development system, incentive and restraint system, talent evaluation system, personnel training system, and labor relation management system. On this basis, the Bank optimizes other related management systems, implements system contents and requirements in practical activities, and thus deepens the reform of human resources.

(4) The Bank enhances human resources management, properly develops talent attraction channels, and recruits necessary talents for business development and internal control management. Diversified employer vocational development accesses are improved, taking position ranking evaluation and appointment as an effective motivation so as to broaden the access of vocational development.

(5) The Bank enhances risk management, and the risk identification and evaluation system which meets the demands of circumspect supervision and business development is perfected. Besides, the Bank reinforces the research and application of various technologies and methods of risk management, and strengthens the construction of IT risk management team, thus further improving the risk management level.

(6) The Bank pays attention to firmly establish value contribution concept and efficiency consciousness, and further improves the ability of identifying common compliance problems and key risk points. At the same time of strengthening compliance management, hypercorrection and risk enlargement which may hinder business development shall be avoided. The Bank works hard for making supervisory requirements, compliance management and business development balance with each other.

(7) The Bank further promotes the combination of operation risk prevention and case prevention. Aiming at credit business, accounting settlement and counter service, billing service, HR management of key positions and other 8 important businesses and 57 key business operations, the Bank requires all departments to find out existing problems and deeply analyze the causes. Moreover, the Bank rationalizes internal control system and business operation procedures, takes effective promoting and rectifying measures according to specific problems, intensifies rectification measures and advances risk control to prevent any risk.

## 第六节 股东大会情况简介

### Introduction to Shareholders' General Meeting

### 6.1 2010年年度股东大会召开情况

#### Annual Shareholders' General Meeting

2010年4月30日，公司董事会在《证券时报》和本行网站上登载了《重庆银行股份有限公司关于召开二○○九年度股东大会年会的通知》

2010年5月21日，在本行19楼多功能厅召开了2009年年度股东大会。出席本次股东大会的31名股东和股东代理人共代表有表决权的股份数为1,864,418,258股，占总股本2,020,618,604股的92.27%。会议的通知、召集、召开以及表决程序均符合《公司法》和《公司章程》的规定。

2009年年度股东大会以记名投票的方式审议通过了以下议案：《董事会2009年度工作报告》、《监事会2009年度工作报告》、《2009年度财务决算报告》、《2009年度利润分配方案》、《2010年度财务预算方案》、《关于童海洋先生辞去本行董事的议案》、《关于选举丁世录先生为本行董事的议案》、《关于延长“首次公开发行境内人民币普通股（A股）股票并上市的方案”的有效期的议案》、《关于延长“申请公开发行A股股票上市过程中有关授权事项”有效期的议案》、《关于续聘天健正信会计师事务所为本行2010年度审计师及增加审计服务内容的议案》、《关于再次修订<重庆银行股份有限公司章程（上市版）>的议案》、《关于拟发起设立兴义万丰村镇银行有限公司的议案》、《关于购置重庆银行解放碑支行营业办公用房的议案》。

重庆伊斯特律师事务所对本次会议召集和召开程序、出席会议股东的资格、提案和表决程序等事项的合法、合规性进行了现场见证，并出具了法律意见书。

On April 30th, 2010, Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd. published the “Notice of Holding 2009 Shareholders’ General Meeting by the Board of Directors of Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd.” on Securities Times.

On May 21, 2010, The 2009 Shareholders' General Meeting was held at the multifunction hall of the Bank on the 19F. There were 31 shareholders and entrusted agents, whose shareholding reached 1,864,418,258 shares, which was 92.27% of the total (2,020,618,604), attended the meeting, which was in accordance with the requirements of Company Law of the People's Republic of China and Articles of Association.

2009 Shareholders' General Meeting reviewed and approved following proposals by disclosed ballot: 2009 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors, 2009 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors, 2009 Final Financial Account Report, 2009 Annual Profit Distribution Plan, 2010 Financial Budget Plan, Proposal of Mr. Tong Haiyang's Resignation as Director of the Bank, Proposal of Appointing Mr. Ding Shilu as Director of the Bank, Proposal of Prolonging the Validity of "Initial Public Offering of Common Stock (A Share) in China", Proposal of Prolonging the Validity of "Authorization Issues in Application for Public Offerings of A Shares and Listing", Proposal for the Continued Contract with Ascenda Certified Public Accountants as the Bank's 2010 Auditor and Increasing Audit Service Items, Proposal of Revising "Articles of Association of Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd. (for Listing)", Proposal of Establishing Village & Town Bank of Xingyi Wanfeng Co., Ltd., and Proposal of Office & Business Occupancy Purchase of Bank of Chongqing Jiefangbei Branch.

Chongqing East Law Firm witnessed the validity and compliance in terms of the procedures of

convening and holding the Shareholders’ General Meeting, shareholders’ qualification and the procedures of proposal and voting, and issued legal opinion.

### 6.2 临时股东大会召开情况

#### Extraordinary General Meetings

#### 6.2.1 股东大会第一次临时会议

## 1st Extraordinary General Meeting

2010年12月15日，公司董事会在《证券时报》和本行网站上登载了《重庆银行股份有限公司关于召开二○一○年第一次临时股东大会的通知》。

2010年12月29日，在本行19楼多功能厅召开了2010年第一次临时股东大会。出席本次股东大会的30名股东和股东代理人共代表有表决权的股份数为1,820,801,833股，占总股本2,020,618,604股的90.11%。会议的通知、召集、召开以及表决程序均符合《公司法》和《公司章程》的规定。

2010年第一次临时股东大会以记名投票的方式审议通过了以下议案：《关于修订<重庆银行股份有限公司章程>的议案》、《关于股东大会对董事会授权的议案》、《关于支付西安分行营业用房购置款项的议案》、《关于重庆银行新大楼建设方案及投资概算的议案》、《关于丁世录先生辞去重庆银行股份有限公司董事的议案》、《关于选举重庆银行股份有限公司第四届董事会成员的议案》、《关于选举重庆银行股份有限公司第四届监事会成员的议案》、《关于重庆银行领导班子薪酬管理办法的议案》、《关于核定执行董事、职工监事2009年度薪酬的议案》。

重庆伊斯特律师事务所对本次会议召集和召开程序、出席会议股东的资格、提案和表决程序等事项的合法、合规性进行了现场见证，并出具了法律意见书。

On December 15, 2010, Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd. published the “Notice of Holding 2010 1st Extraordinary General Meeting by the Board of Directors of Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd.” on Securities Times.

On December 29, 2010, 2010 1st Extraordinary General Meeting was held at the multifunction hall of the Bank on the 19F. There were 30 shareholders and entrusted agents, whose shareholding reached 1,820,801,833 shares, which was 90.11% of the total (2,020,618,604), attended the meeting, which was in accordance with the requirements of Company Law of the People's Republic of China and Articles of Association.

2010 1st Extraordinary General Meeting reviewed and approved following proposals by disclosed ballot: Proposal of Revising "Articles of Association of Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd. (for Listing)", Proposal of General Meeting's Authorization to the Board of Directors, Proposal of Payment for Business Occupancy of Bank of Chongqing Xi'an Branch, Proposal of Construction Plan and Investigation Budget of New Office Building, Proposal of Mr. Ding Shilu's Resignation as Director of the Bank, Proposal of Electing Members of Fourth Board of Directors of Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd., Proposal of Electing Members of Fourth Board of Supervisors of Bank of Chongqing Co., Ltd., Proposal of Remuneration Management Regulation for Leadership of Bank of Chongqing and Proposal of 2009 Remuneration Verification of Executive Directors and Supervisors.

Chongqing East Law Firm witnessed the validity and compliance in terms of the procedures of convening and holding the Shareholders’ General Meeting, shareholders’ qualification and the procedures of proposal and voting, and issued attorney opinion letter..

## 第七节 董事会报告

## Report of the Board of Directors

### 7.1 管理层讨论与分析

Management Discussion & Analysis

本公司主营业务范围主要包括：吸收公众存款；发放短期、中期和长期贷款；办理国内结算；办理票据贴现；发行金融债券；代理发行、代理兑付、承销政府债券；买卖政府债券；从事同业拆借；提供信用证服务及担保；代理收付款项及代办保险业务；提供保管箱业务；信贷资产转让业务；办理地方财政周转金的委托贷款业务。外汇存款；外汇贷款；外币兑换；国际结算；结汇；售汇；同业外汇拆借；外汇担保；外汇买卖；买卖除股票以外的外币证券；资信调查、咨询、见证业务以及经中国银行业监督管理委员会批准的其他业务。

Principal operating businesses: absorption of deposits; short-term, medium-term and long-term loan business; domestic settlements; discounting; issuance of financial bonds; acting as distribution agent, payment agent and underwriter of government bonds; trading government bonds; inter-bank lending/borrowing; L/C services and bank guarantee; payment/collection agent and insurance agent; safe deposit box service; credit assets transfer; consignment loan of local fiscal revolving funds; foreign currency deposit, loans; foreign currency exchange; international settlements; purchase and sale of foreign currencies; inter-bank foreign currency lending/borrowing; foreign currency guarantee and trading; foreign currency securities trading except for stock; credit investigation, inquiry and certification; and other businesses under the approval of CBRC.

#### 7.1.1 总体经营情况回顾

##### Review of General Operation

2010年是公司进入结构性战略调整的起始之年，全行员工团结一心，奋力拼搏，以高度的责任感和主人翁精神，加快结构调整、业务转型的步伐，实现了又快又好的发展。

2010 was the starting year of structural strategy adjustment. All staffs worked wholeheartedly with high responsibility and sense of ownership, speeding up structural adjustment and business transformation and finally realizing rapid and excellent development.

1、资产规模进一步扩大，盈利能力进一步提升。资产总额1,082.39亿元，同比增加273.73亿元，增幅33.85%，完成董事会下达经营计划的110.45%；各项存款余额738.56亿元，同比增加146.08亿元，增幅24.66%，完成董事会下达经营计划的103.05%；全口径贷款余额529.68亿元，同比增加107.13亿元，增幅25.35%，完成董事会下达经营计划的102.65%；实现利润总额13.90亿元，同比增加2.67亿元，增幅23.79%；实现净利润10.83亿元，同比增加2.07亿元，增幅23.65%，完成董事会下达经营计划的106.80%。

2、对地方经济建设的支持力度进一步加大。一是在确保风险控制前提下，积极支持地方重点基建

项目建设。二是贯彻落实有扶有控信贷政策，加强对市场主导项目领域信贷支持力度。三是加强金融同业合作，积极搭建同业合作平台，推动银团贷款业务发展。

3、业务结构调整初战告捷。本行2010年对重庆市中小企业贷款的增量超过本行成立以来的总和。中小企业业务创新力度进一步加强，初步摸索出以市场需求为先导，促进产品流程再造，以新产品推广为契机，发现和培育新市场的创新路径。

4、收入结构趋向合理。中小业务贷款综合回报收益进一步提高，市场议价能力有所增强；是西南地区第一家获得基金代销业务资格的城商行；代理保险业务发展势头良好，实物黄金业务取得有效进展；中间业务收入增量超额完成年度目标。

5、监管指标全面达标。在“融资平台清理”等多项重大监管政策出台后，本行相关管理部门采取严格、及时、妥善、灵活的应对措施，各项清理整改工作取得实效。流动性、中长期等重点监管指标均符合监管要求。

6、社会形象大幅提升。成功举办了全国城市商业银行发展论坛第十次会议，顺利接待中央和地方多位领导来行考察调研。

7、人力资源开发取得突破。员工职业发展通道初步建立，骨干人才培养计划启动实施，年金计划顺利启动，多层次，多类别的培训项目取得实效，建立了业务骨干信息库。

8、跨区域发展和全覆盖工作有序推进。贵阳分行开业，西安分行获准筹建。同时大力推进全覆盖战略，截止2010年年末，在全市范围内共设有网点83个，覆盖全市34个区县。

9、内控工作继续巩固深化。合规手册圆满编制完成，全面风险管理项目顺利启动。“内控建设年”、“服务满意度建设年”等活动深入开展，全员合规意识进一步加强，内控案防水平进一步提高。

1. Stable growth of assets and improved profitability. During the reporting period, the total assets of the Bank reached RMB 108.239 billion, increasing by RMB 27.373 billion with a growth rate of 33.85% compared to that of previous year and 110.45% achieving the objective of the operating plan set by the Board of Directors. The deposit balance and loan balance respectively amounted to RMB 73.856 billion and RMB 52.968 billion, increasing by RMB 14.608 billion and RMB 10.713 billion. The growth rate respectively reached 24.66% and 25.35% with 103.05% and 102.65% operating plan accomplished as set by the Board of Directors. Total profits and retained profits respectively amounted to RMB 1.390 billion and 1.083 billion, increasing by RMB 0.267 billion and RMB 0.207 billion with growth rate of 23.79% and 23.65%, which accomplished by 106.80% as set by the Board of Directors.

2. Increasing support to local economic construction. First was to actively support the construction of local key infrastructural projects on condition that risks were properly controlled. Second was to implement supportive but controlled credit policy and give support to credit aid of market-oriented projects. Third was to reinforce cooperation with business partners in the same financial field, actively set up cooperation platform and thus promote the development of syndicated loan service.

3. Initial success in adjustment of business structure. Increment of Chongqing SME loans in 2010 exceeded the total increments since establishment. The Bank enhanced business innovation of SME service and initially find out an innovative way, i.e. to promote process reengineering driven by market demands and to create and cultivate new market with the opportunity of new product promotion.

4. More reasonable revenue structure. Comprehensive returns of SME loans were improved and market negotiation ability was strengthened. The Bank, as urban commercial bank, first obtained the qualification of funds commissioned agency in Southwest China. Insurance agent services maintained a

good momentum of development, and gold business also made headway. Besides, incremental revenue of intermediary business outperformed the annual goal.

5. Supervision target reached the standard. Since "Financing Platform Cleaning" and many other significant policies were issued, the Bank took strict, timely, proper and flexible measures to accomplish cleaning and rectification. Key supervision indexes such as fluidity and longer term were in conformity with supervisory requirements.

6. Substantially increasing social image. The Bank successfully held 10th National Urban Commercial Bank Forum, and showed hospitality to central and local leaders who visited for investigation and survey.

7. Breakthrough in HR development. Employer vocational development access was initially built and key personnel training plan had been put into implementation. Annuity plan started smoothly. Multi-level and multi-class training projects achieved good results. The Bank also set up an information database of key business personnel.

8. Cross-regional & Overall Development was advanced orderly. Guiyang Branch opened for business, and Xi'an Branch was qualified to construct. Meanwhile, the Bank gave great impetus to boost overall strategy. By 2010, there had been 83 branches in Chongqing, covering 34 districts and counties.

9. Continuous intensified internal control. Compliance handbook was successfully compiled and comprehensive risk management project started smoothly. "Year of Internal Control", "Year of Service Satisfaction Construction" and other activities were carried out in a deep-going way. Compliance concept of every staff and the level of internal control were improved.

#### 7.1.2 主要业务经营情况

Business Overview

## （一）公司银行业务

(I) Corporate Banking

公司业务保持了良好的发展势头。报告期末，公营存款余额620.12亿元，比上年新增131.91亿元，增幅27.02%；公营一般贷款余额365.42亿元，比上年新增83.48亿元，增幅为29.61%。

The Bank kept rapid development in the reporting year. At the year end, the corporate deposit balance amounted to RMB 62.012 billion, increasing by RMB 13.191 billion with a growth rate of 27.02%; the corporate loan balance reached RMB 36.542 billion, increasing by RMB 8.348 billion with a growth rate of 29.61%.

1、积极推动公营存贷款业务稳定较快发展。加强了对公营存款的动态分析监控，公营存款日均保持稳定增长趋势。公营贷款营销力度加大，科学制定信贷投放计划，均衡控制贷款投放节奏，加快信贷结构调整。

2、积极推动中小企业业务发展。报告期末，全行中小企业贷款余额83.4亿元，较年初增长48.2亿元，完成全年进度的160.7%，不良率0.69%。与全市39家担保机构建立了良好的合作关系。

3、积极稳妥推进信贷结构调整。积极支持地方重点基建项目，贯彻落实有扶有控信贷政策，培育新的信贷增长点；加强金融同业合作，培育供应链金融业务增长点。

4、强化公司业务宏观管理与规划指导。以“强化宏观管理，加强营销指导”为核心，结合区域差

异、分支行差异，科学拟订全年发展计划。加强中长期指标控制与集团客户管理，积极推进业务规划与储备工作。

5、加强公司业务营销团队建设工作。修订完善相关制度，加强团队建设制度保障。推动客户经理绩效考核系统优化升级和公司客户经理绩效改革。

6、加强公司业务重点产品开发与营销推动。对原有公司类产品进行集中梳理，加强业务模式创新，加强与重庆本地同业、银行业协会、外地同业的联系和沟通，积极延伸信贷领域合作，推动银团贷款业务发展。

1. Stable and rapid development of corporate deposit and loan business. The Bank strengthened monitoring of corporate deposit dynamic analysis, thus ensuring steady increasing trend of daily corporate deposits. Marketing efforts on corporate deposit were enhanced and scientific credit supply plan was established. Besides, the Bank appropriately controlled the rhythm of credit supply and accelerated the adjustment of credit structure.

2. Promotion of the SME service. In the end of the reporting year, the SME loan balance reached RMB 8.34 billion, increasing by RMB 4.82 billion which accomplished annual plan by 160.7% with reject ratio of 0.69%. The Bank had established good cooperative relationship with 39 guarantee agents in Chongqing.

3. Reliable adjustment of credit structure. The Bank supported key local infrastructure construction, implemented supportive but controlled credit policies, and created new credit growth points. Besides, through cooperating with financial partners, growth points of financial business in supply chain were cultivated.

4. Reinforcement of business macro-management and plan guidance. Taking "reinforcing macro-management, strengthening marketing guidance" as core, and integrating differences of regions and branches, the Bank scientifically drafted the annual plan. The plan focused on intensifying longer-term index control and management of group customers as well as promoting business plan and reservation task.

5. Reinforcement of constructing the marketing team. Related regulations were revised and perfected, and regulations for team construction were guaranteed. The Bank also propels optimization and upgrade of customer manager performance evaluation system as well as performance reformation of customer managers.

6. Enhancement of key product development and marketing promotion. The Bank collected the former products and reorganized them; besides, intensified innovation of business mode. Moreover, the Bank contacted and communicated with local and nonlocal financial partners and banking associations, expanding cooperation in credit loan and promoting development of syndicated loan service.

## （二）个人银行业务

(II) Personal Banking

报告期末，个人储蓄存款118.45亿元，全年新增14.17亿元，增幅13.59%，个人贷款余额达88.70亿元，全年新增44.36亿元，增幅100.05%；累计发行长江借记卡155.9万张，全年新增17.06万张，增幅12.3%；累计发行信用卡17,156张，全年新增9,918张，信用卡循环信贷总额达2.85亿元，全年新增1.55亿元，增幅达119.23%。

At the end of the reporting period, the personal deposit balance amounted to RMB 11.845 billion with an increase of RMB 1.417 billion and by 13.59%. The personal loan balance was RMB 8.870 billion, rising by RMB 4.436 million with a growth rate of 100.05%. The issued Changjiang Card accumulated to 1.559 million with an increase of 170.6 thousand and by 12.3%. The number of issued credit cards reached 17,156, rising by 9,918. The revolving loan of credit cards totalized RMB 285 million with an increase of RMB 155 million and by 119.23%.

1、以银行卡发行创新促进储蓄存款增长。购买卡通形象版权，开创银行卡发行三方合作模式，引入健康、低碳、环保公益的绿色金融服务理念，抓住最具创造力和消费力的客户群体，促进优质客户群体稳存增存。

2、积极推动个人消费类贷款规模化发展。成立个贷审批中心，完成各分支行个贷动态授权，推进个贷审批体制改革，优化审批流程，提高专业化的个贷审批效率和风险控制能力。

3、完善中间业务产品体系。增加基金代销业务，加快代理保险业务发展，实现银保手续费收入为2009年的5.2倍。

4、统筹推进客户信息系统（ECIF）和零售客户经理绩效考核系统建设，加强零售客户经理团队建设。制定适合本行零售业务发展的客户经理、大堂经理及驻点人员管理制度，制定科学的考核激励方案，充分调动客户经理的积极性。

1. Promoting deposit increase by innovatively issuing new cards. The Bank purchased copyright of cartoon images and created a new mode in card issuance, i.e. 3-party cooperative issuance. Healthy, low carbon and environmental-friendly concept of financial service was introduced, thus grasping creativity and consumptive ability of customers, and ensuring steady and increasing deposits of high quality customer groups.

2. Promoting the development of personal consumptive loan in a proper scale. The Bank set up the personal loan accreditation center, accomplished dynamic authorization of personal loan in branches, promotes reformation of personal loan accreditation system, optimized approval procedures and improved professional personal loan accreditation efficiency and ability for risk control.

3. Perfecting the product system of intermediary business. The Bank increased funds distributing service and speeding up the development of insurance agent services. The handling charges of bancassurance reached 5.2 times of the results in 2009.

4. The Bank improved ECIF and construction of performance evaluation system of retail customer managers with an overall planning and enhanced team construction of retail customer managers. Management regulations for retail customer managers, lobby managers and staffs in branches had been established, which was suitable to the actual situation of the Bank. Besides, scientific evaluation and incentive plan was made to sufficiently mobilize the initiative of customer managers.

## (三) 资金业务

(III) Treasury Operations

报告期末，资金业务在有效控制风险的前提下，全面完成各项业务指标，实现考核利润1.18亿元，比上年度增长近45%。

At the final of the reporting period, the capital business, under the precondition of effective risk

control, had completely fulfilled the various business indexes, realizing the assessment profit of RMB 118 million with a growth of nearly 45% than last year.

1、全年发行理财产品约46亿元，比去年同期新增24亿元，增幅109%。实现中间业务收入逾3000万元。

2、债券结算代理业务快速增长。2010年我行债券结算代理业务实现零突破，结算代理量1600亿元，实现中间业务收入约800万元。

3、加强流动性管理，提高资金管控能力。与大型国有银行建立了良好的合作关系，保证融资渠道的畅通。积极配合全行流动性管理的需要，低成本吸收中长期存款20亿元。

4、积极寻找新的客户群，拓展异地银团贷款，新增银团贷款2亿元。

1. The year-round financing products issued were about RMB 4.6 billion, increasing by RMB 2.4 billion than the same time last year, with a growth of 109%. Realization of intermediary business was over RMB 30 million.

2. Bond payment agency business grew rapidly. In 2010, the bank achieved a breakthrough in bond payment business, with the amount of settlement agency of RMB 160 billion and the realization of the income of intermediary business of RMB 8 million.

3. The liquidity management had been strengthened and the capital control ability had been improved. The bank had established good relations of cooperation with large state-owned banks to ensure unblocked financing channels. Actively cooperating with the need of over-bank liquidity management, the bank absorbed RMB 2 billion of long-term savings at low cost.

4. The bank actively looked for new client base, expanded distance syndicated loan, and added RMB 200 million of syndicated loan.

## (四) 中间业务

(IV) Intermediary Business

报告期末，中间业务收入2.28亿元，同比增长43.54%；中间业务净收入1.98亿元，同比增长51.48%，其中：支付结算类业务收入3,852万元，银行卡业务收入1,049万元，理财业务收入3,475万元，担保及承诺类业务收入926万元，咨询顾问类收入13,462万元。

At the end of the reporting period, the income of intermediary business was RMB 228 million, with an increase of 43.54%. The net income of intermediary business was RMB 198 million, with an increase of 51.48%, including: income of payment settlement category of RMB 38.52 million, income of bank card business of RMB 10.49 million, income of financing business of RMB 34.75 million, income of guarantee and commitment business of RMB 9.26 million, and income of consulting of RMB 134.62 million.

## (四) 科技建设

(V) Scientific Construction

2010年是本行实施三年IT战略规划的关键一年，大力提升了IT发展能力和保障能力，对业务发展起到较好支撑作用，保障了系统的安全稳定运行。

2010 was a critical year of the 3-year IT strategic planning. In this year, the bank had vigorously promoted the IT developing ability and safeguard ability, providing a good supporting for the business development and ensuring safe and stable function of the system.

1、大力推进制度体系建设。参照银监会《商业银行信息科技风险管理指引》以及业界最佳实践，对现有管理制度进行梳理，完善制度体系，确保各个环节有章可循，增强工作的规范性。

2、为有效支撑未来业务发展，启动了上丁新数据中心的建设工作与下一代网络的建设工作。完成东泉中心的机房建设工作，同时启动东泉灾备中心DWDM网络设计工作，两地三中心基础设施初具雏形，为有效提高灾难发生时业务持续性和数据完整性提供了保障。

3、IT服务管理系统顺利上线运行，实现了IT事件管理、变更管理流程的自动化处理，为今后进一步提高IT服务水平打下了良好基础。

1. System construction was vigorously promoted. Referring to the Guidelines for Information Technology Risk Management of Commercial Banks from CBRC and the best industry practice, the bank combed and perfected the existing management system, ensuring all links are rule-based as well as enhancing the criterion of the work.

2. In order to support future business development, the bank launched the construction work of the new data center in Shangding and the next generation network. The construction work of generator room in Dongquan center had been completed. Meanwhile, the design work of DWDM network in Dongquan disaster preparation center was launched; the rudiment of infrastructure of two places and three centers had appeared, providing security for effectively improving the business continuity and data integrity when disaster happened.

3. The IT service management system had been on operation smoothly. The automation dealing of IT event management and change management process had been realized, laying a good foundation for further improving IT service level.

## (六) 电子银行业务

##### (VI) E-banking Business

网银业务快速发展，管理的有效性和安全性进一步加强。报告期末，网上银行客户较去年新增8360户，其中个人大众版客户新增6713户，个人专业版客户新增1078户，企业客户新增569户，全行网上银行客户累计达到3.38万户；实现网上交易38.25万笔，完成交易金额464.41亿元。

The online banking business was developing rapidly, of which the effectiveness and safety of the management was further enhanced. At the final of the reporting period, the number of online banking client was 8,360 more than last year, among which, 6,713 were Personal mass edition clients, 1,078 were Personal professional clients, and 569 were corporate clients. The number of online banking client had accumulated to 33,800. The bank had realized 382,500 sums of online trade, completing RMB 46.441 billion of the transaction amount.

2、贷款利息收入增加系本公司贷款平均余额较上期增加所致。

3、买入返售金融资利息收入增加系买入返售票据的利率水平和平均余额较上期大幅上升所致。

4、债券投资利息收入增加系债券投资平均余额较上期增加所致。

5、手续费及佣金收入增加系本行加大了对中间业务的推广力度，提高了顾问及咨询手续费收入所致。

Notes: 1. The decrease of interest income from nostro accounts was due to the remarkable reduction of interest rate of nostro accounts.

2. The increase of interest income from loan was due to the increase of loan average balance of the prior period.

3. The increase of interest income from reverse repurchase agreements was due to the dramatic increase of interest rate of reverse repurchase and average balance, compared to the prior period.

4. The increase of interest income from bond investment was due to the increase of average bond investment balance.

5. The increase of fee and commission income was due to the promotion of intermediary businesses which raised the income from advisory and consulting services.

注1：现金及存放中央银行款项变动主要原因系本行吸收存款规模增大引起缴存央行存款准备金增加所致。

注2：买入返售金融资产变动主要原因系本行加大同业间票据回购业务的合作以及本行流动性匹配等因素引起买入返售金融资产余额的增加所致。

注3：应收款项类投资变动主要原因系受本行风险管理、资金运用收益率等管理策略的影响以及本行流动性匹配等因素，本行加大了对其他金融机构理财产品投资总量所致。

注4：同业及其他金融机构存放款项变动主要原因系本行加大与中国邮政储蓄银行有限责任公司等同业机构的合作以及本行流动性匹配等因素引起同业存放款项的余额增加所致。

注5：卖出回购金融资产款变动主要原因系本行受流动性匹配、资金运用收益率等管理策略的影响引起卖出回购金融资产款余额的增加所致。

注6：利息收入变动主要原因系本行贷款平均余额、债券投资和买入返售金融资产业务的规模均较上年有较大增长所致。

注7：利息支出变动主要原因系本行存款平均余额和卖出回购金融资产业务的规模较上年有较大增长所致。

注8：手续费及佣金收入主要系本行中间业务规模较上年增长所致。

注9：资产减值损失变动主要原因系本期受宏观经济、政策影响，加大对融资平台贷款损失准备计提所致。

Note 1: The deposit balance was raised sharply, which triggered the increase of deposit reserve with central banks.

Note 2: The Bank expanded inter-bank cooperation in terms of bill and credit assets repurchase and the balance of reverse repurchase agreements increased due to factors such as liquidity match.

Note 3: Receivables investments changed mainly due to the influence of risk management strategy and yield rate of fund application, and liquidity match and other factors which were caused by the increase of aggregate investment on other financial products.

Note 4: Nostro accounts and deposits of other financial institutions varied because the Bank enhanced the cooperation with financial organizations such as PSBC, and the deposit balance of nostro accounts increased due to liquidity match and other factors.

Note 5: It was affected by management strategies such as liquidity match, capital utilization and return on capital, which caused the increase of repurchase agreement balance.

Note 6: The Bank increased average balance of loans and expanded the business of bond investment and reverse repurchase, what was the reason that interest incomes changed.

Note 7: The Bank increased average balance of deposits and expanded the business of repurchase, what was the reason that interest expense changed.

Note 8: Income from fee and commission was due to the remarkable increase of the intermediary businesses compared to that of previous year.

Note 9: Asset impairments were influenced by macro-economy and policies as well as the fact that the Bank increased the reserves of counting and drawing for loan loss on financing platform.

### 7.2.15不良贷款情况及采取的相应措施

### Non-performing Loans and Applied Countermeasures

不良贷款情况

Non-performing loans

截止2010年12月31日，全行不良贷款余额为18,964.60万元。其中，次级类贷款773.50万元，可疑类贷款12,781.20万元，损失类贷款5,409.90万元。不良贷款余额较年初降低891.40万元。

As of December 31, 2010, the non-performing loan balance was RMB 189.646 million. Amongst, the sub-standard loans amounted to RMB 7.735 million, doubtful loans amounted to RMB 127.812 million and loss loans amounted to RMB 54.099 million. The non-performing loan balance of the Bank was decreased by RMB 8.914 million from the beginning of the year.

## （二）已采取的相应措施

##### Applied Countermeasures

积极推进存量不良贷款的清收工作，2010年共收回1,824.8万元存量不良贷款。朝华科技贷款项目自从与西昌电力达成和解，由其履行补充赔偿责任后，今年第二次履行了还款责任，归还本金400万元；营业部与四川通业建设有限公司借款纠纷一案，今年收回全部贷款本金700万元及相应利息费用；我行申请执行重庆欧艺实业发展有限公司一案，现因对方提出协商和解，暂缓执行。

The Bank actively pushed forward the liquidation of non-performing loans. In the year 2010, non-performing loans amounted to RMB 18.248 million was taken back. Settlement has been made between the Bank and the guarantor (Xichang Electric Power Co., Ltd) upon the load dispute of Zarva Technology (Group) Co., Ltd.. Since the transferring of all compensation responsibilities, the guarantor has fulfilled his second time compensation responsibility this year. Written off bad loans amounted to RMB 4 million were recovered in the year. In the case of loan dispute between Banking Department and Sichuan Tongye Construction Co., Ltd., total loan principal payments amount to RMB 7 million and its relevant interest expenses were recovered. In the case where the Bank applied for the loan performance of Chongqing Ouyi Industrial Development Co., Ltd., the performance is suspended for the counterparty proposed settlement by agreement.

## 7.3 面临的主要风险及相应对策

### Risks and Corresponding Measures

作为一家区域性的城市商业银行，公司在经营中面临的主要风险包括信用风险、流动性风险、汇率风险、利率风险、操作风险以及信息科技风险。

报告期内，本行在普华永道等外部专业咨询机构的协助下，制定了全面风险管理体系规划，从风险管理战略与政策、风险治理架构、风险管理流程、风险计量和内部控制体系等方面，明确了未来五年内全面风险管理蓝图及改进方向。

As a regional municipal commercial bank, the Bank encounters many risks affecting the operation of the bank, such as credit risk, liquidity risk, currency risk, interest rate risk, operational risk and information technology risk, etc.

During the compilation of this report, the Bank has established a planning of overall risk management system with the assistance from external professional advisory services like PWC. The system helps the Bank to get a clear and defined idea of the blueprint for overall risk management of the next five years and its directions for improvements, from the aspects of risk management strategies and policies, risk governance framework, risk management process, risk measurement and internal control system, etc.

### 7.3.1 信用风险

#### Credit Risk

信用风险是指因客户或交易对手未按约定条款履行其义务的风险。本行承担信用风险的资产包括各项贷款、拆放同业和买入返售资产、存放同业款项、银行账户债券投资、应收利息、其他应收款和表外资产，其中最主要的是各项贷款和表外资产。

报告期内，通过授信指引科学调整结构，合理配置信贷资源，着力优化行业及期限结构，大力推动中小企业信贷业务，信贷结构调整初见成效。一是通过资源投入和政策倾斜来大力发展中小企业和个人按揭贷款业务，全年中小企业信贷总量及个人按揭贷款实现翻番，促进了业务转型；二是从强化项目筛选、完善风控措施等环节入手，稳妥把握项目贷款的投放，有效降解信贷风险；三是加强对公司类中长期贷款的管理，优化信贷期限结构。

本行深入贯彻落实银监会“三个办法一个指引”，积极开展对政府融资平台贷款的清理工作。根据银监会“三法一指引”精神，及时出台了《固定资产贷款管理暂行办法》、《流动资金贷款管理暂行办法》、《个人贷款管理暂行办法》，着重做好贷款受托支付方式的落地工作；按照“逐包打开、逐笔核对、重新评估、整改保全”的监管要求，扎实做好融资平台自查，对政府融资平台贷款进行分析定性，实施分类管理，通过项目剥离、增加有效担保等方式，及时化解潜在风险。

继续完善信贷监控模式，推行标准化的检查流程，加大对信贷管理薄弱环节和信贷业务风险的跟踪力度。2010年，坚持审慎分类原则，至报告期末信用风险不良资产余额2.16亿元，不良资产率0.23%，不良贷款余额1.90亿元，不良贷款率0.36%。授信集中度和关联度指标均符合监管要求。

Credit risk results from the failure of a borrower or of a borrowing party in the fulfillment of related obligations as agreed. Financial assets facing the credit risks were reverse repurchase agreements, Nostro accounts, bonds investments, interest receivable, other receivables and off-balance-sheet items. Primary financial assets facing the credit risk are loans and off-balance-sheet items.

During the compilation of this report, the Bank addressed itself to the problems of scientific structure adjustments guided by granting credit, rational allocation of credit resources, optimization of industry and term structure, uttermost promotion of credit operations for minor enterprises. The structural adjustment of credit has achieved initial success. One of the measures the Bank took was to develop credit operations for minor enterprises and personal mortgage loan through resource input and policy support. The annual credit amount of minor enterprises and personal mortgage loan were doubled, which facilitated the transformation of business. The second measure was to work on the reinforcement of project selection and the perfection of risk management measures, control the supply of project loan reliably, and degrade credit risk effectively. Another measure was to enhance the management of medium- and long-term loans to corporations and optimize credit term structure.

The Bank thoroughly applied CBRC's "Three Methods, One Guideline" policy. The recover work of loans from government financial platform was carried out vigorously. According to the spirits of CBRC's "Three Methods, One Guideline", the Bank issued Interim Procedures for Fix Asset Loans Management, Interim Procedures for Liquidity Loans Management and Interim Procedures for Personal Loans Management. Great emphases were attached to the ground work of ensuring entrusted payment method. The Bank also steadily performed financing platform self-examinations in accordance with CBRC's regulatory requirement of "opening pack by pack, verifying trade by trade, appraise again and again, make sure to rectify and reform". Qualitative analyses were carried on loans from government financing platform. The Bank implemented systematic management and reduced potential risks by separating projects, increasing effective guarantees, etc.

The Bank will continue on perfecting credit monitoring mode, pushing forward standardized inspection processes and further strengthening the tracking of weak links in credit management as well as risks in credit operation. In 2010, the Bank stuck to the principle of prudent classification. The balance of non-performing assets facing credit risk amounted to RMB 216 million at the end of reporting period, with the non-performing assets ratio of 0.23%. The balance of non-non-performing loan amounted to RMB 190 million, with the non-performing loan ratio of 0.36%. The concentration ratio of credit and loan, as well as the association index, were all in accordance with the related standard.

## 7.3.2 流动性风险 Liquidity Risk

流动性风险是指在履行与金融负债有关的义务时遇到的资金短缺的风险。流动性风险包括因无法履行还款责任，或者因无法及时和/或以合理的价格为本行资产组合变现提供资金所带来的风险。

董事会根据风险偏好制定了与流动性风险整体管理相关的政策、策略、程序、限额及应急计划，高级管理层下设资产负债管理委员会负责执行流动性风险管理日常工作，计划财务部、资金部及其它业务部室相配合，形成分工协作、职责分明、运行高效的流动性风险管理体系。

本行不断提高流动性管理方面的信息系统应用水平，通过系统实时监控流动性指标及流动性敞口情况，形成计量流动性风险的自动化手段及定期监控机制，并根据流动性敞口状况协调全行资产负债业务，通过内部资金转移定价，积极主动的调整资产负债期限结构。本行不断改善流动性管理手段，建立了资产负债管理周会制度，加强和完善制度建设，及时进行政策调整，将流动性水平调控到适当的水平之上。

2010年，国家采取积极的财政政策和适度宽松的货币政策，对信贷投放实施窗口指导，同时，为控制通胀预期，央行全年上调六次存款准备金率和两次加息以回收市场流动性和控制通货膨胀。本行流

动性管理以合理配置资金、支持业务发展为主，加大了资金在信贷业务、资金业务及债券业务上的配置力度，合理控制备付率，提升整体资产收益率。在加大资产配置力度同时，加强了存款的可持续增长与稳定性工作，调整负债结构，开拓多方面的负债来源，在积极提升负债稳定性同时，合理控制负债平均成本。报告期内，本行资产负债业务平稳健康发展，流动性状况良好。

此外，本行建立了流动性风险压力测试方案，定期对流动性风险开展压力测试。下一步还将持续优化测试方案，以强化对流动性风险的监测及预警。

LLiquidity risk is capital shortage risk encountered in the fulfillment of financial liability related responsibility. Liquidity risk results from the failure in the fulfillment of repayment or failure in providing funds for the realization of asset portfolio of the Bank in time or at a reasonable price.

The board of directors established policies, strategies, procedures, quotas and crash projects in terms of risk preference. Assets and Liabilities Conservator Organization was established as subordinate body of upper management layer to carry out the routine work of liquidity risk management. Finance and Accounting Department, Fund Department and other operation departments worked together, forming a highly efficient liquidity risk management system of all parties cooperating on basis of the clear distribution of responsibilities and roles.

The Bank kept on improving the application of liquidity risk management related information system. Real time monitoring was performed on liquidity scale and liquidity open position, as an automatic tool and a regular monitoring mechanism to measure liquidity risk. Meanwhile, assets and liabilities operation of the Bank was coordinated in line with liquidity open position. The Bank took the initiative to regulate its assets and liabilities term structure by the transferring and pricing of internal funds. As a result of the continuous improving on liquidity management tools, the Bank was able to establish a weekly meeting system of assets and liabilities management. The system was further strengthened and perfected. Certain policies were adjusted timely and the liquidity situation was regulated above proper level.

In 2010, China adopted proactive fiscal policy and moderately easy monetary policy. Window guidance was provided for credit supplies. At the same time, People's Bank of China raised reserve requirement ratio six times and interest rate two times throughout the year to control inflation expectation, recover market liquidity and curb inflation. The Bank's liquidity management mainly focused on rational fund allocation and business development supporting. Funds allocated to credit operations, treasury operations and bond operations were increased. The reserve requirement ratio was rationally controlled and the overall return on assets was raised. While enhancing the allocation of funds, the Bank further strengthened the sustainable growth and stabilization of deposit business. Liability structure was regulated, multi-source liabilities were developed. The Bank managed to control the average cost of liabilities within a rational range while improving liability stability positively. During the reporting period, assets and liabilities business of the Bank is developing steadily and healthily. The liquidity is also in good condition.

In addition, the Bank established pressure testing plan for liquidity risk, and carried out pressure tests for liquidity risk periodically. The Bank will further perfect the testing plan in future, thus to strengthen the monitoring and pre-warning for liquidity risk.

## 7.3.3 汇率风险

### Exchange Rate Risk

汇率风险是指由于外币资产负债币种错配和外币交易导致的货币头寸错配而产生的风险。

本行加强对国际市场汇率走势的实时监控及预测分析，及时调整汇率应对策略。对外汇买卖及结售汇敞口头寸进行严格管理，各外币敞口头寸实行总额控制并及时进行敞口平盘，各项指标均严格控制在监管机构规定的范围内。此外，公司暂未开展衍生品业务和以盈利为目的的投机性自营外汇买卖业务，有效降低了汇率风险。

Currency risk results from the currency mismatch of foreign currency assets and liabilities, as well as cash position mismatch caused by foreign currency trade.

The Bank strengthened the real-time monitoring and analysis on the interest rate of the global market, and made proper adjustment to the corresponding measures. Strict management of authorization, credit and open position was carried out in respect of foreign exchange transactions and settlement and sales of foreign exchange. Open position sum of foreign currencies was controlled. All indexes were strictly controlled within the required range of regulatory bodies. In addition, the Bank hasn't currently carried out any financial derivative business or self-support foreign exchange trade, which effectively reduced the exchange rate risk.

#### 7.3.4 利率风险

##### Interest Rate Risk

利率风险是指金融资产和金融负债的公允价值或未来现金流量因市场利率变动而发生波动的风险。

本行加强对负债成本、资产盈利和市场利率的变动的综合分析，及时调整资产负债结构，规避风险。利用人民币利率风险管理系统来量化、识别、预警可能到来的人民币利率风险，监控和管理资产和负债组合的整体利率风险，并运用内部资金转移定价系统引导全行资产负债结构的优化调整。

Interest rate risk refers to the change of fair value of financial assets and financial liabilities, and the change of future cash flow, as due to the fluctuation of market interest rate.

The Bank strengthened the comprehensive analysis on the liability cost, the assets benefit and the fluctuation of market interest rate, and timely adjusted the assets and liabilities structure, thus to avoid any possible risk. RMB interest rate risk management system is adopted to forecast any possible RMB interest rate risk. In addition, the Bank supervised and managed the overall interest rate of assets and liabilities, and guided the optimization and adjustment of assets and liabilities structure by using the internal capital transferring and pricing system.

## 7.3.5 操作风险

##### Operational Risk

操作风险是指由不完善或有问题的内部程序、员工和信息科技系统，以及外部事件所造成损失的风险。

报告期内，本行持续推动并优化滚动式常态化检查机制、创新合规管理手段，不断强化操作风险管理。一是针对授信、会计两个业务条线，编写完成合规手册，实现业务规章制度的具体化、标准化和流程化，切实提高规章制度的可操作性；同时建立与之相配套的合规风险评估体系，并依托新开发的合规风险管理系统，为本行搭建了一个上下沟通的合规管理科技平台，促进合规管理工作更加规范地展开。二是实现对分支机构全覆盖的滚动检查，重点做好“强化后续整改”、“统一检查报告”、“统一检查标准”三个环节的工作，并对滚动检查进行持续调整和优化，建立操作风险敏感指标体系，为监控操作风险易发环节、定位高风险业务和机构、合理配置检查资源起到了较好的支撑作用。

Operational risk is caused by the imperfect or flawed internal procedures and staff & information technology system, as well as damages rising from external issues.

During the reporting period, the Bank continued on promoting and optimizing the normalized rolling inspection mechanism, innovating compliance management manual, and strengthening operation risk management. Firstly, the Bank compiled the compliance manual for operation lines of credit granting and accounting. Operation rules and regulations were materialized, standardized and streamlined, which greatly increased the manipuility of the rules and regulations. Meanwhile, a corresponding compliance risk assessment system was established. A compliance management technology platform for superiors and subordinates to communicate was set up based on the newly-developed compliance risk management system. Compliance management was carried out more normatively. Secondly, the Bank implemented full coverage rolling inspections on the branches. Emphases were attached to the links of "strengthening follow-up rectifying and reforming work", "unifying inspection report", "unifying inspection criterion". The rolling inspection system was continuously adjusted and optimized. Operational risk sensitive indicator system is set up to better support the monitoring of operational risk tended links, the positioning of high-risk businesses & organizations and the rational allocation of inspection resources.

## 7.3.6 信息技术风险

信息科技风险是指银行在运用信息科技过程中，由于自然因素、人为因素、技术漏洞和管理缺陷产生的操作、法律和声誉等风险。

报告期内，本行持续完善IT治理架构，在开展与监管要求、先进银行最佳实践之间的差距评估的基础上，进一步明确了董事会、高级管理层、相关部门的IT风险管理职责和工作机制。

为不断提高IT风险管理能力，保障整个IT系统的平稳运行，通过本地冗余系统配备、异地灾备中心有效运转和开展多次应急演练，有效保障了业务持续性，全年未发生造成重大业务损失的IT突发故障；重视信息安全，逐步完善安全策略体系，加强对网络和信息系统的安全控制；规范IT项目管理流程，加强对开发项目的跟踪督导；实施对外包商的入围风险评估，强化外包风险控制。

Information technology risk refers to the operational, legal and honorary risk due to the natural factor, human factor, technical defect and management effect during the use of information technology.

During the reporting period, the Bank kept on perfecting its IT governance structure, further defined the IT risk management responsibilities and working mechanism of the board of directors, upper management layer and relevant departments on the basis of difference evaluation carried out against regulatory requirements and the best practices of advanced banks.

To further improve its IT risk management ability and secure the smooth running of the entire IT system, the Bank equipped itself with local redundant system and effectively operating allopatric disaster recovery data center. Multiple emergency drillings were also carried out to effectively secure the sustainability of the Bank's business. No IT catastrophic failure causing major business losses happened this year. Furthermore, the Bank attached great important to information security. The security policy system was gradually perfected and security controls over network and information systems were strengthened. Meanwhile, IT project management process was regulated. Tracking and supervision on development projects were strengthened. Risk assessments were carried out upon chosen outsourcers. Control over outsource risks was intensified.

# 7.4 经营环境、宏观政策的变化及影响

## Change of Business Environment and Policies and Related Influences

2011年，是国家第十二个五年发展计划的起始之年，也是机遇之年。抓住发展机会，完善管理，调整结构是本行今年工作的重中之重。

The year of 2011 is the starting point of China's Twelfth Five-year Development Plan. This is also the year of opportunity. Seizing the opportunities for development, perfecting management and adjusting structure are the Bank's top priorities of this year.

1、国际国内经济金融形势的机遇和挑战。未来三年，世界经济将继续呈现差异化复苏态势，重点需要关注欧洲债务危机演变和新兴经济体资产泡沫风险。国内方面，中国经济将主要运行在经济周期的繁荣阶段，工业化和城镇化进程加快，经济增长结构的优化成为政府工作的重点。

2、十二五规划带来的机遇和挑战。十二五规划建议明确提出，消费将逐步成为经济增长的主要驱动力，可以预见传统的“出口—投资驱动经济”增长模式正发生积极变化。同时，政府将致力于战略性新兴产业的发展、加速内陆地区的城市化进程、加大保障性住房建设力度以及鼓励支持民间投资。

3、市场客户需求变化带来的机遇和挑战。对公客户总体金融需求依然乐观，但结构上呈现差异化趋势。零售客户总体信贷需求增速稳中有降，投资理财等其它金融服务需求继续趋升，结构上的差异化特征更为明显。

4、中国银行业自身发展所带来的机遇和挑战。未来三年存款负债难度加大，信贷增速将显著放缓，盈利能力保持稳定，盈利结构有望改善。资本压力与日俱增，高资本消耗模式难以为继。

1. Opportunities and Challenges from the financial situations of international and domestic economy: In the next three years, world economy will continue to present a differentiated recovery state. The focal point will be on the evolvement of European debt crisis and asset bubble risk of emerging economies. In the aspect of domestic economy, China will mainly move forward in the prosperity phase of economic cycle. The industrialization and urbanization will accelerate. The optimization of economic growth structure becomes the key point of government work.

2. Opportunities and Challenges from the Twelfth Five-year Plan: the Twelfth Five-year Plan clearly stated that consumption is gradually become the main driven force of economic growth. It is predictable that the growing mode of "Exportation-Investment driven Economy" is changing positively. Meanwhile, the government will embark on the developing strategic emerging industries, accelerating the urbanization progress of inland area, intensifying the construction of indemnificatory housing and encouraging private investment.

3. Opportunities and Challenges from the change in market & customer demand: the financial demand of corporate customers is still promising but will present differentiation tendency in structure. The increasing of overall credit demand of retail customers will be stable with a slight decline. Their need for other financial services like investment and financing continues to ascend, but the structural differentiation will be more obvious.

4. Opportunities and Challenges from the self-development of China's banking industry: In the next three years, more difficulties will appear for the business of deposit and liability. Credit operation acceleration is slowing down notably. The Bank's profitability will remain stable and its profit structure is likely to be improved. Pressures of capital grow day by day. The mode of high capital consumption can hardly survive.

### 7.5 新年度业务发展计划

### Business Plan of the Coming Year

1、总体思路：对照董事会未来发展战略规划和“十二五”规划关于金融体系建设的总体目标，继续坚持以科学发展观为统领，以上市为契机，以创新为主题，以转型为主线，以员工福祉为导向，以加快结构调整为抓手，以组织架构优化和流程再造为动力，以形成业务发展和内部管理双向内生机制为支撑，全力打造重庆银行核心竞争力，增强持续经营能力，为全市经济社会发展和金融中心建设做出新的更大贡献。

1. Overall Concept: In accordance with the development strategy of the Board of Directors and the overall objective of the financial system construction concerned by “the Twelfth Five-year Plan”, the Bank will make every effort to build our core competitiveness, strengthen our sustainable operation ability and make new greater contribution for Chongqing economic society development and the financial center construction, adhering to the guidance of the Scientific Outlook on Development, seizing the opportunities given by the listing, concentrating on innovation and transformation and aiming at employees’ welfare, with the structure adjustment acceleration as the backer, the organization structure optimization and flow re-building as the engine, and the business development & inner management bidirectional mechanism formation as the supporter.

# 2、工作措施

2. Main Work Measures of 2010

##### (1) 业务创新能力建设

(I) The Building of Business Innovation Capability

公司业务方面：系统规划公司业务产品，力推供应链金融，大力支持实体经济、中小企业领域、战略性新兴产业的信贷需求。做好对“十大民生工程”的配套金融服务工作，切实履行应尽的社会责任。在“两翼”农户万元增收工程、微小企业创业贷款、绿色信贷业务上做出亮点。抓紧推出农村三权抵押贷款新产品。发展本行作为牵头行、代理结算行的银团贷款业务。加强融资平台、房地产、“两高一剩”等重点领域风险控制。

零售业务方面，以银行卡发行创新促进储蓄存款增长。培育优质客户群体，强化中高端客户维护；着力调整个人贷款结构，对优质开发商、优质楼盘的按揭业务进行资源倾斜，形成住房、汽车按揭全面发展的良好局面。继续完善中间业务产品体系，实现中间业务手续费收入大幅增长，成立财富管理中心，打造“长江财富通”综合理财服务品牌。

资金业务方面，票据转贴现业务量和交易对手要保持良好增长势头，尤其要做好与分支行联动工作，把转贴现业务规模进一步做大。

In the aspect of Bank business: the Company will systematically organize its business products, promoting supply chain finance and greatly support the credit demands of substantial economy, minor enterprises and strategic emerging industries. Auxiliary financial services shall be provided for "the Top Ten Civil Engineering" to fulfill the Bank's due social responsibility. Furthermore, the Bank plans to attain remarkable achievements on the "Two Wings" Income Increasing Projects for Farmers, business start-up loans for micro-enterprises and Green Credit Operations. The new product of mortgage loans for three kinds of property rights in rural area will be introduced soon. Syndicated loan operations with goals of making the Bank as initiator and agent settling banks will be developed. Risk controls in key areas like

the financing platform, real estate and highly polluting and energy intensive resource enterprises will be strengthened.

In the Aspect of retail business, the Bank will promote the growth of saving deposit by innovating the issuing of bank cards. High quality customer groups must be cultivated. The maintenance of medium- and high-end customers shall be intensified. In addition, the Bank will embark on the adjustment of personal loan structure, allocating more resources to the mortgage business of high quality developers and houses, forming a favorable situation of integrated development in mortgages in both housing and autos. The Bank will keep on perfecting middleman business product system, increasing fee incomes for middleman business by a large margin. Wealth management centers will be set up. The Bank aims to forge the integrated financial servicing brand of "Long River TenPay"

In the aspect of capital business, the Bank's bill rediscount business shall maintain the growth momentum as its counterparties do. Joint operations with branch banks shall be especially taken care of, to further enlarge the Bank's bill rediscount business.

### (2) 政策管理能力建设

(II) The Building of Policy Management Capability

着力培育制度政策自我完善更新的内生动力。通过对政策和制度的梳理，以及对现有组织架构的审视，把全行管理工作提升到一个新水平。推进系统清理工作并搭建全行政策体系框架，进一步明确总行部门职能定位。改进内部管理流程，激发员工的责任感和主人翁意识，全面提升管理精细化水平。

The Bank will spend time and effort cultivating its inner driving forces for the self-perfection and self-renewing of systems and policies. The management work of the whole Bank will be elevated to a new level through combing of the Bank's policies and system, as well as inspection on the current organization structure. System cleaning will be pushed forward and a policy system framework applicable to the whole company will be set up. The functions of different departments in the head office will be further clarified. The inner management processes are improved to inspire the sense of responsibility and ownership in employees. Refined management will be promoted comprehensively.

# 3、资源配置能力建设

# 3. The Building of Resource Allocation Capability

优化绩效管理体系，准确传导和分配绩效压力，将全行每一岗位都纳入价值创造的流程。探索按业务条线和机构两个纬度的考核模式，加大存贷比考核力度，充分发挥劳动竞赛对稳存增存的激励作用。科学配置信贷资源，加强中长期贷款指标额度管理。

The Bank will optimize the performance management system, transmitting and distributing tensions of performance accurately downstream. Each position in the Bank will be counted as one part of the value creation process. The performance evaluation mode of business line related and organization related will be explored. More importance will be attached to the evaluation of deposit ratio to fully bring out the stimulation effect labor competitions will have on stabilizing and increasing deposit business. Furthermore, the Bank will allocate credit resources scientifically and enhance the management on the index quota of medium- and long-term loans.

# 4、内控案防能力建设

# 4. The Building of Internal Control and Case Prevention Capability

信贷风险管理要夯实制度、架构、系统三大支柱，建立和完善一系列信贷标准，提高信贷风险控制的创新能力。开展平安重庆银行建设试点示范工作，做好合规手册和合规风险管理系统的行内推广。进一步探索建立分支行合规员管理机制。逐步深化和完善合规风险评估工作。根据三级架构管理模式，结合监管部门的要求，对现行治安保卫内控制度进行梳理、修订、完善，多层面强化员工的安全教育培训。

Policy, framework and system are the three pillars supporting the work of credit risk management and will thus be solidly established. The Bank will establish a series of credit criteria and keep perfecting them. The innovation capability of credit risk control will also be developed. In addition, the pilot project of Building a Safe Chongqing Bank will be carried out. Compliance manuals and compliance risk management system will be promoted within the Bank. Further exploration on building compliance management mechanism for branch banks will be proceeded. The compliance risk assessment work will be gradually deepened and perfected. According to the three level management mode and the requirements from regulatory bodies, the Bank will carry out combing, revising and perfecting work on the current security guarding and inner controlling systems. Safety educational trainings to the employees will also be strengthened from multiple aspects.

# 5、人才队伍能力建设

# 5. The Capability Buildings of Workforces

进一步深化人力资源和培训管理工作，构建和完善人力资源管理体系。理顺总行与分支机构之间的关系，进一步加强总行作为规划和指导中心的功能定位。推行人员编制管理，完善多元化的员工职业发展通道。全面推行企业年金、补充医疗保险，为员工建立多层次、多支柱、自助性的福利保障体系。强化人力资源培训工作，开展全行综合管理条线员工在人力资源管理制度上的培训，着重做好大学生入职培训、培养期员工和业务骨干培训。

The Bank will further improve human resources work and its management and training, and will establish and keep perfecting the human resources management system. The relationship between head office and each branch will be clarified to further emphasize the head office's function as planning and directing centre. Manning management will be promoted, and the diverse employee career developing channel will be perfected. In addition, the Bank will comprehensively implement enterprise annuity and supplementary medical insurance, building a diverse, multi-pillar supported and autonomic welfare security system for employees. Moreover, human resource training work will be intensified. The Bank will carry out training on human resources management systems for employees on comprehensive management lines all over the Bank. Emphases shall be attached to the induction training of newly-graduated students, as well as the training of core employees and stuffs to be cultivated.

本行2010年度财务报告经天健正信会计师事务有限公司审计，注册会计师石义杰、杨柳出具了“天健正信审（2011）GF字第030007号”标准无保留意见的审计报告。

The 2010 financial Statement was audited by Ascenda Certified Public Accountants, and was signed by certified accountant Shi Yijie and Yang Liu. The Audit Report (TJZXS [2011] GF No. 030007) with clean audit opinion was issued.

## 7.7 董事会日常工作情况

Routine Work of the Board of Directors

### 7.7.1 董事会日常工作情况

Routine Work of the Board of Directors

1、2010年2月9日，第三届董事会第三十八次会议在本公司召开。应到董事14人，实到董事12人，委托董事2人，会议审议通过了《关于2009年度审计报告的议案》、《关于三年财务报表及附注的议案》、《关于2009年度内部控制自我评价报告的议案》、《关于2009年度财务决算的议案》、《关于2009年度利润分配方案的议案》、《关于2010年度财务预算方案的议案》、《关于2010年度资产负债管理策略的议案》、《关于2010年度风险管理策略的议案》、《关于2009年度董事会费用执行情况及2010年度董事会费用预算的议案》、《关于成立董事会信息科技指导委员会的议案》，以上议案的表决结果均为14票一致同意。

会议审议通过了《关于核定2009年度领导班子成员薪酬的议案》，该议案的表决结果为同意11票，马千真董事长、甘为民董事、谷德荣董事因对该事项有重大利害关系，回避表决。

本行5名独立董事分别就《关于2009年度利润分配方案的议案》和《关于核定2009年度领导班子成员薪酬的议案》发表了“同意”的独立意见。

会议还听取了《2009 年度风险监测报告》、《2009 年度内部审计工作报告》、《2009 年度经营工作情况及述职报告》、《重庆银监局对本行2009 年经营管理情况的监管意见》、《2010 年董事会工作要点》、《关于2010 年度经营计划指标按审计后数据予以调整的报告》、《关于拟在贵州省设立村镇银行的报告》和《高级管理层成员2009 年度述职报告》。

On February 9, 2010, the 38th meeting of the Third Board of Directors was held in the Bank. The quorum of the meeting shall be 14. There were 12 directors and 2 director representatives attending the meeting. The following proposals were discussed and approved at the meeting: Proposal for the Audit Report of 2009, Proposal for the Three-year Financial Statement and Appendix, Proposal for the Internal Control Self-evaluation Report of 2009, Proposal for the Financial Accounting of 2009, Proposal for the Profit Distribution Plan of 2009, Proposal for the Financial Budget Program of 2010, Proposal for the Assets and Liabilities Management Strategies of 2010, Proposal for the Risk Management Strategies of 2010, Proposal for the Implementation of Board Expense 2009 and the Budget of Board Expense 2010, and Proposal for Establishing IT Steering Committee of the Board of Directors. All proposals were supported by 14 directors unanimously.

Proposal for the 2009 Remuneration of the Management was discussed and passed. The proposal was supported by 11 directors. Chief Executive Ma Qianzhen, Director Gan Weimin and Director Koo Tak Wing avoided the voting due to significant interest association.

Five independent directors of the Bank independently “agreed” with the Proposal for the Profit Distribution Plan of 2009 and the Proposal for the 2009 Remuneration of the Management.

In addition, 2009 Risk Monitoring Report, 2009 Internal Auditing Report, Operation Condition and Work Report of 2009, Report on the Inspection Opinion of CBRC Chongqing Office on the Management

of the Bank of 2009, 2010 Key Points of Work of the Board of Directors, Report on the Adjustment of 2010 Operating Plan Targets in Accordance with Audited Data, Report on the Establishing Plan of Rural Banks in Guizhou Province and 2009 Executive Management Work Report, were reported at the meeting.

2、2010年3月16日，第三届董事会第三十九次会议以传签方式召开。应参与传签的董事14人，实际表决的董事14人。会议审议通过了《关于设立重庆银行西安分行的议案》、《关于向重庆交通旅游投资集团有限公司集团客户授信60000万元的议案》、《关于向重庆南方集团有限公司集团客户续授信8000万元的议案》，以上议案的表决结果均为14票一致同意。

会议审议通过了《关于向重庆水利投资（集团）有限公司集团客户授信30000万元的议案》，该议案的表决结果为同意13票，吴家宏董事因与该审议事项存在重大利害关系，回避表决。

本行5名独立董事分别就《关于向重庆交通旅游投资集团有限公司集团客户授信60000万元的议案》、《关于向重庆南方集团有限公司集团客户续授信8000万元的议案》和《关于向重庆水利投资（集团）有限公司集团客户授信30000万元的议案》发表了“同意”的独立意见。

On March 16, 2010, the 39th meeting of the Third Board of Directors was held in the way of circular signature. The quorum of the meeting shall be 14. There were 14 directors attending the meeting. Proposal for the Establishment of Chongqing Bank Xian Branch, Proposal for Customer Credit Granting to Chongqing Traffic and Tourism Investment Group Co., LTD. with Amount to RMB 600 Million, Proposal for Credit Granting to Chongqing South Group with Amount of RMB 80 million, were discussed and approved at the meeting. The above mentioned proposals were supported by all 14 directors unanimously.

Proposal for Customer Credit Granting to Chongqing Water Conservancy Investment (Group) Co., Ltd. with Amount to RMB 300 Million, was discussed and passed at the meeting. The proposal was supported by 13 directors. Director Wu Jiahong avoided the voting due to significant interest association.

Five independent directors of the Bank independently “agreed” with the Proposal for Customer Credit Granting to Chongqing Traffic and Tourism Investment Group Co., LTD. with Amount to RMB 600 Million, the Proposal for Credit Granting to Chongqing South Group with Amount of RMB 80 million and the Proposal for Customer Credit Granting to Chongqing Water Conservancy Investment Group Co., Ltd. with Amount to RMB 300 Million respectively.

3、2010年4月29日，第三届董事会第四十次会议在本公司召开。应到董事14人，实到董事13人，委托董事1人。会议审议通过了《2009年年度报告及摘要》、《2009年度社会责任报告》、《2009年度董事会工作报告》、《关于董事会对2009年度董事履职情况评价的报告》、《关于续聘天健正信会计师事务所为本行2010年度审计师及增加审计服务内容的议案》、《关于修订2010年度经营计划指标和财务预算方案的议案》、《关于拟发起设立兴义万丰村镇银行有限公司的议案》、《关于购置西安分行营业办公用房的议案》、《关于购置解放碑支行营业办公用房的议案》、《关于设立重庆银行成都乐山分行及购买营业网点的议案》、《关于2010年网点发展计划的议案》、《关于重庆银行声誉风险管理办法（试行）的议案》、《关于再次修订<重庆银行股份有限公司章程（上市版）>的议案》、《关于延长“首次公开发行境内人民币普通股（A股）股票并上市的方案”有效期的议案》、《关于延长“申请公开发行A股股票并上市过程中有关授权事项”有效期的议案》、《关于召开2009年度股东大会年会的议案》，以上议案的表决结果均为14票一致同意。

会议还听取了《关于第39次董事会议案传签审议结果的通报》、《关于本行高级管理人员2009年度述职评价结果的通报》、《关于本行第三届董、监事2009年度薪酬执行情况的通报》、《2010年第
